Top-agent orchestrator with tool-based subagents
---
## Problem

Issue #13 conflated model routing with agent architecture and proposed brittle heuristic-based agent selection. The hardcoded `selectAgent()` approach fails when:
- A single message contains mixed intent (e.g. "what did we decide about auth?" + "I'm thinking we should add SSO")
- The right action requires sequential reasoning (research first, then delegate)
- New capabilities are added (every new "agent mode" means new routing rules)

## Goal

Implement a **single orchestrating agent** that dispatches work to specialized subagents. The **knowledge graph is the communication bus** — agents read from and write to the graph, and never pass data to each other directly. The orchestrator controls sequencing and synthesizes the user-facing response, but does not shuttle data between agents.

## Architecture

```
User
  │
  ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────┐
│   Top Agent (Thin Orchestrator)  │  ← Decides who to call, synthesizes response
│   Model: Sonnet (default)        │
└─────┬───┬───┬───┬───┬───┬──────┘
      │   │   │   │   │   │
      ▼   ▼   ▼   ▼   ▼   ▼
   ┌────┐┌────┐┌────┐┌────┐┌────┐┌────┐
   │Res.││Des.││Eng.││Mkt.││PM  ││Ext.│  ← Subagents (read/write graph independently)
   └──┬─┘└──┬─┘└──┬─┘└──┬─┘└──┬─┘└──┬─┘
      │     │     │     │     │     │
      ▼     ▼     ▼     ▼     ▼     ▼
   ┌──────────────────────────────────┐
   │        Knowledge Graph            │  ← Communication bus
   │  (decisions, tasks, questions,    │
   │   observations, features,         │
   │   learnings, entities)            │
   └──────────────────────────────────┘
```

## Two Paths to the Graph

| Source | How it gets to the graph | Why |
|--------|------------------------|-----|
| **User messages** | Extraction pipeline (Haiku) infers entities from unstructured text | User input is unstructured — needs LLM to parse intent and extract entities |
| **Agent output** | Agent writes directly to graph | Agent created the data — it already has the structured form, nothing to "extract" |

Agents never go through the extraction pipeline. They have direct graph write access and are responsible for persisting their own entities (questions, tasks, observations, decisions).

## Graph as Communication Bus

**All agent-to-agent communication happens through the graph.** The orchestrator does NOT pass Agent A's output to Agent B. Instead:

1. Orchestrator decides which agent(s) to invoke
2. Each agent reads the graph for context it needs
3. Each agent writes its outputs (entities, observations, decisions) directly to the graph
4. Subsequent agents read the graph and see prior agents' work
5. Orchestrator synthesizes the user-facing response from graph state

```
User: "We need to launch feature X"
  → Orchestrator calls Research agent
  → Research reads graph, writes observations + context entities
  → Orchestrator calls Engineering agent
  → Engineering reads graph (sees Research's observations), writes tasks
  → Orchestrator calls Marketing agent (parallel with Engineering)
  → Marketing reads graph (sees Research's observations), writes tasks
  → Orchestrator reads graph state, synthesizes response
```

**Why this matters:**
- Agents are fully decoupled — Engineering doesn't know Research exists, it just reads the graph
- Works across sessions — agent runs tomorrow, picks up observations from today
- No orchestrator bottleneck — it doesn't need to understand every agent's output format
- Agents can react to ANY agent's work, not just what was explicitly wired
- Adding a new agent requires zero changes to existing agents

### Orchestrator (Thin)

The orchestrator's only job:
1. **Decide** which agent(s) to invoke for the current user message
2. **Sequence** when order matters (Research before Engineering)
3. **Parallelize** when agents are independent (Engineering + Marketing)
4. **Synthesize** the user-facing response from graph state

It does NOT:
- Pass data between agents
- Manage agent side effects
- Understand agent-specific output formats
- Do bookkeeping on behalf of agents

### Subagents

Each subagent has direct graph access and owns its own side effects. Subagents are stateless — they read context from the graph, do their work, write results back to the graph.

| Subagent | Purpose | Model | Writes to graph |
|----------|---------|-------|-----------------|
| **Research** | Graph queries, context gathering, past decision lookup | Haiku | Observations |
| **Design Partner** | Clarifying questions, assumption challenging, product co-design | Sonnet | Questions, observations |
| **Engineering** | Task breakdown, technical analysis, dependency mapping | Sonnet | Tasks, observations |
| **Marketing** | Positioning, messaging, go-to-market, content | Sonnet | Tasks, observations |
| **Sales** | Outreach, demos, pipeline, qualification | Sonnet | Tasks, observations |
| **Product Manager** | Task dedup/merge, project status, dependency tracking, priority management (#45) | Haiku | Tasks (create/update), features, observations |
| **Extraction** | Entity/relationship extraction from **user messages only** | Haiku | All entity types (inferred from user text) |
| **Deep Analysis** | Complex multi-step reasoning when needed | Opus | Decisions, observations |

### Shared Tools

All subagents share common tools:
- `suggest_tasks` — propose tasks, routed through PM agent for dedup/merge (#43)
- `search_entities` — read from graph
- `create_observation` — write observations for other agents (#38)
- Direct graph write tools for questions, decisions, etc.

### Key Patterns

**Sequential (order matters):**
```
Research writes to graph → Engineering reads graph
Orchestrator controls the sequence, but doesn't pass data
```

**Parallel (independent):**
```
Engineering + Marketing both read from graph simultaneously
Both write their outputs, no conflicts
```

**Cross-session:**
```
Session 1: Design Partner writes observation "user uncertain about pricing model"
Session 2: Sales agent reads observation, factors into outreach strategy
No orchestrator involved — the graph persists between sessions
```

**Mixed intent:**
```
User: "What did we decide about auth? Also I'm thinking we add SSO"
  → Orchestrator calls Research (reads graph for auth decisions)
  → Orchestrator calls Design Partner (reads graph for context, explores SSO)
  → Both write to graph independently
  → Orchestrator synthesizes response
```

**Task suggestions through PM:**
```
Any agent calls suggest_tasks → PM agent deduplicates/merges → refined suggestions to user
```

## Extraction: User Messages Only

The extraction pipeline runs for every **user message** to infer entities from unstructured text. It does NOT process agent output — agents write their own entities directly.

Extraction is always-on and runs in parallel with whatever the orchestrator does conversationally.

## Model Selection

Model choice is an **implementation detail of each subagent**, not a top-level routing concern:
- Orchestrator: Sonnet (good balance of reasoning + speed)
- Lookup/extraction/PM subagents: Haiku (fast, cheap)
- Deep analysis: Opus (rare, only when orchestrator determines high complexity)

## Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] Single thin orchestrator handles all user messages
- [ ] Orchestrator decides sequencing and parallelization, does NOT pass data between agents
- [ ] Subagents read from and write to the graph independently
- [ ] Subagents own their own side effects — direct graph writes, no extraction step
- [ ] Extraction pipeline processes user messages only, not agent output
- [ ] Agent-to-agent communication is entirely through graph entities
- [ ] Agents can discover other agents' work across sessions via graph reads
- [ ] Extraction runs as a parallel pipeline for every user message
- [ ] Model selection is per-subagent, not a top-level routing decision
- [ ] Shared tools available to all subagents
- [ ] Telemetry captures: subagent invocations, model used, latency, cost per message

## Out of Scope
- User-facing agent/model picker
- ML-based orchestration (orchestrator uses LLM reasoning, not learned routing)
- Direct agent-to-agent calls (all communication through graph)

## Related
- #42 — Entity categories
- #43 — Shared suggest_tasks tool (routes through PM)
- #45 — Product Manager agent
- #38 — Observation entity (agent-to-agent signals)
- #39 — Post-execution reconciliation
- #40 — @mention dispatch + inline streaming
- #44 — Agent learnings
- #30 — Governance feed

## Replaces
Closes #13

Observation entity for async agent-to-agent communication via graph
---
## Problem

Agents need to communicate cross-cutting concerns to each other — things that aren't decisions, tasks, or questions but that other agents should know about. Currently there's no entity type for this. Without it, agents either lose these signals (ephemeral orchestrator output) or force them into the wrong entity type (creating a "decision" for something that's really just a warning).

## Goal

Add an `observation` entity type that serves as the lightweight signal layer in the graph. Agents write observations directly when they notice something relevant to other agents or the human. Other agents discover these observations by reading the graph — no orchestrator mediation needed.

## Examples

| Agent | Observation | Severity | Category |
|-------|------------|----------|----------|
| Engineering | "Auth implementation will delay launch by ~2 weeks" | warning | engineering |
| Research | "Competitor X just launched a similar feature at half the price" | warning | marketing |
| Design Partner | "User seems uncertain about pricing model — revisit before committing" | info | sales |
| Engineering | "Feature A and Feature B have conflicting database requirements" | conflict | engineering |
| Marketing | "No launch messaging exists for the feature shipping next week" | warning | marketing |
| Sales | "Three prospects asked for SSO — consider prioritizing" | info | engineering |

## Schema

```sql
DEFINE TABLE observation SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD text ON observation TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD category ON observation TYPE option<string>
  ASSERT $value IN ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] OR $value IS NONE;
DEFINE FIELD source_agent ON observation TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD severity ON observation TYPE string
  ASSERT $value IN ["info", "warning", "conflict"];
DEFINE FIELD status ON observation TYPE string
  ASSERT $value IN ["open", "acknowledged", "resolved"]
  DEFAULT "open";
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON observation TYPE datetime DEFAULT time::now();
DEFINE FIELD resolved_at ON observation TYPE option<datetime>;
```

### Graph relationships

```sql
-- Observation belongs to a project
DEFINE TABLE observation_belongs_to TYPE RELATION IN observation OUT project SCHEMAFULL;

-- Observation relates to specific entities it's about
DEFINE TABLE observation_about TYPE RELATION IN observation OUT task | decision | question | feature SCHEMAFULL;

-- Observation was triggered by a message
DEFINE TABLE observation_from TYPE RELATION IN observation OUT message SCHEMAFULL;
```

## Severity Levels

| Severity | Meaning | Governance feed tier |
|----------|---------|---------------------|
| `conflict` | Two things contradict, human resolution likely needed | Red (blocking) |
| `warning` | Risk or gap identified, should be addressed soon | Yellow (important) |
| `info` | Contextual signal, useful for awareness | Green (awareness) |

## Agent Tool

```typescript
{
  name: "create_observation",
  description: "Record an observation about something you noticed that other agents or the human should know about. Use for cross-cutting concerns, risks, gaps, or signals that don't fit as a decision, task, or question.",
  parameters: z.object({
    text: z.string().describe("What you observed — specific and actionable"),
    severity: z.enum(["info", "warning", "conflict"]),
    category: z.enum(["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"]),
    about: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("Entity IDs this observation relates to"),
    project: z.string().optional(),
  })
}
```

Agents write observations directly to the graph — no extraction step, no user approval. Observations are agent-internal signals. The human sees them through the governance feed (#30).

## How Agents Consume Observations

Agents read open observations as part of their graph context loading:

```sql
-- Get open observations relevant to current project
SELECT text, severity, category, source_agent, created_at
FROM observation
WHERE status = "open"
  AND (->observation_belongs_to->project CONTAINS $project OR category = $agent_category)
ORDER BY
  severity = "conflict" DESC,
  severity = "warning" DESC,
  created_at DESC
LIMIT 20;
```

Agents should factor open observations into their work:
- Engineering agent sees "auth will delay launch by 2 weeks" → adjusts task estimates
- Marketing agent sees "competitor launched similar feature" → adjusts positioning
- Sales agent sees "user uncertain about pricing" → prepares pricing comparison

## Observation Lifecycle

```
open → acknowledged → resolved
```

- **open**: just created, visible to all agents and governance feed
- **acknowledged**: human or agent has seen it, still needs resolution
- **resolved**: addressed — the concern was handled (task created, decision made, risk mitigated)

Observations can be resolved by:
- Human acknowledging in the governance feed
- An agent creating a task/decision that addresses the concern
- The condition no longer being true (e.g. the delay was resolved)

## Governance Feed Integration (#30)

Observations feed directly into the governance feed:
- `severity: "conflict"` → Red tier (needs human resolution)
- `severity: "warning"` → Yellow tier (review when ready)
- `severity: "info"` → Green tier (awareness)

## Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] `observation` table in SurrealDB schema with all fields
- [ ] Graph relationships: `observation_belongs_to`, `observation_about`, `observation_from`
- [ ] `create_observation` tool available to all subagents
- [ ] Agents write observations directly to graph (no extraction step)

Product Manager agent: task dedup/merge, project status, feature and dependency tracking
---
## Problem

Multiple agents suggest tasks independently. Without a central authority, duplicates pile up, existing tasks don't get enriched with new context, and nobody tracks cross-agent task conflicts or project-level status. Features also need lifecycle management — breaking down into tasks, tracking completion, and surfacing blockers.

## Goal

A **Product Manager agent** that is the single authority on tasks, features, and project state. All task suggestions from other agents route through the PM for dedup, merge, and lifecycle management.

---

## Responsibilities

| Domain | What PM does |
|--------|-------------|
| **Task dedup/merge** | When agents suggest tasks, PM checks graph for existing matches. Merges if similar, discards duplicates, passes through new ones. |
| **Task lifecycle** | open → in_progress → completed. PM updates status and tracks transitions. |
| **Feature management** | Features break down into tasks. PM tracks feature completion based on child task status. |
| **Project status** | Aggregates task/decision/question/feature state per project. Answers "what's the status of project X?" |
| **Dependency tracking** | Which tasks/features block which. PM surfaces blocked items. |
| **Priority management** | Re-prioritizes based on new information, observations, and cross-agent signals. |
| **Cross-agent awareness** | Knows what all agents have suggested/created. Single source of truth for work items. |

## Task Dedup/Merge Logic

When processing task suggestions from `suggest_tasks` (#43):

| Graph state | Suggested task | PM action |
|-------------|---------------|-----------|
| No similar task exists | "Implement JWT middleware" | Pass through as new suggestion for user approval |
| Open task, same intent, minor diff | "Implement JWT auth middleware" vs existing "Implement JWT middleware" | Update existing task with richer title/context, notify user |
| Open task, same intent, new scope | "Implement JWT middleware with refresh token rotation" | Update existing task to include new scope, notify user |
| Closed/completed task, same intent | "Implement JWT middleware" | Pass through as new — previous work done, this is new scope |
| Exact duplicate of open task | "Implement JWT middleware" | Discard silently |

### Similarity check

```sql
-- Find potentially matching open tasks
SELECT id, title, category, priority, status
FROM task
WHERE status IN ["open", "in_progress"]
  AND (->task_belongs_to->project CONTAINS $project OR $project IS NONE)
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

PM agent uses semantic judgment (not just string matching) to determine if a suggestion matches an existing task.

## Feature Management

Features are higher-level work items that decompose into tasks:

```
Feature: "User Authentication"
  ├── Task: "Implement JWT middleware" (engineering)
  ├── Task: "Design login page" (design)
  ├── Task: "Research OAuth providers" (research)
  └── Task: "Write auth documentation" (operations)
```

PM responsibilities for features:
- Track which tasks belong to which feature
- Calculate feature completion % from child task status
- Surface features with all tasks completed (ready for review)
- Surface features with blocked tasks
- Create observations when feature timelines are at risk

```sql
-- Feature completion status
SELECT
  id, title, status,
  count(<-belongs_to<-task WHERE status = "completed") AS completed_tasks,
  count(<-belongs_to<-task) AS total_tasks,
  <-belongs_to<-task[WHERE status = "open" AND ->depends_on->task[WHERE status != "completed"]] AS blocked_tasks
FROM feature
WHERE ->feature_belongs_to->project CONTAINS $project;
```

## PM Agent Definition

```typescript
{
  name: "product_manager",
  model: "haiku",  // fast, cheap — mostly graph queries + structured reasoning
  tools: [
    "search_entities",
    "create_observation",
    "update_task",
    "create_task",
    "update_feature",
    // graph read/write tools
  ],
  system_prompt: `
You are the Product Manager agent. You are the single authority on tasks, features,
and project status.

## Task Suggestions
When you receive task suggestions from other agents:
1. Search the graph for existing tasks with similar intent
2. If an open task matches: update it with new context/scope, return as "updated"
3. If an exact duplicate of an open task: discard, return as "discarded"
4. If a closed task matches: this is new work, return as "new suggestion"
5. If no match: return as "new suggestion" for user approval

## Feature Tracking
- Monitor feature completion based on child task status
- Create observations when features are at risk (blocked tasks, missing tasks)
- When all tasks for a feature are completed, surface for review

## Project Status
When asked for project status, aggregate:
- Tasks by status and category
- Features by completion %
- Open questions and pending decisions
- Active observations (warnings, conflicts)

## Dependencies
- Track task-to-task and feature-to-feature dependencies
- Surface blocked items proactively via observations
- When a blocking task is completed, check if downstream items can proceed
`
}
```

## PM Response Format (for suggest_tasks)

```typescript
type PMResult = {
  suggestions: SuggestedTask[];                              // new tasks for user approval
  updated: { taskId: RecordId; title: string; changes: string }[];  // existing tasks enriched
  discarded: { title: string; reason: string }[];            // duplicates dropped
};
```

Frontend renders:
- **New suggestions** → TaskSuggestion cards with Accept/Dismiss
- **Updated tasks** → inline notification: "Updated existing task: [title] → [changes]"
- **Discarded** → not shown (silent dedup)

## Invocation Patterns

### Via suggest_tasks (internal)
Any agent calls `suggest_tasks` → PM processes → returns refined list.
PM is invoked automatically, not by user @mention.

### Via orchestrator (explicit)
User asks "what's the status of project X?" → orchestrator invokes PM → PM queries graph → returns status report.

### Via @mention
User: "@product what's blocked right now?" → PM queries dependencies → returns blocked items.

### Proactive (via observations)
PM can be invoked periodically or by the orchestrator to check project health:
- Any features with all tasks done but not marked complete?
- Any tasks blocked for more than N days?
- Any features missing tasks (no child tasks)?

## Testing

**Unit tests:**
- Exact duplicate → discarded
- Similar open task with new info → updated
- Similar closed task → new suggestion
- No match → new suggestion
- Feature completion calculation
- Blocked task detection

**Integration:**
- Agent suggests duplicate task → PM merges → user sees update notification
- Agent suggests new task → PM passes through → user accepts → entity created
- Two agents suggest similar tasks in same batch → PM deduplicates
- All feature tasks completed → PM surfaces for review

## Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] PM agent defined with system prompt, tools, and Haiku model
- [ ] Task dedup: exact duplicates discarded
- [ ] Task merge: similar open tasks updated with new context
- [ ] Task passthrough: genuinely new tasks forwarded for user approval
- [ ] Closed tasks treated as new scope, not merged
- [ ] Feature completion tracking from child task status
- [ ] Dependency tracking: blocked tasks/features surfaced
- [ ] Project status aggregation across all entity types
- [ ] PM creates observations for at-risk features and stale blocked items
- [ ] PM invocable via orchestrator, @mention, and internally via suggest_tasks

## Related
- #36 — Orchestrator (PM is a subagent)
- #43 — suggest_tasks tool (routes through PM)
- #42 — Entity categories (PM reports by category)
- #38 — Observations (PM writes risk/blocker observations)
- #39 — Post-execution reconciliation (PM observations checked in reconciliation)
- #40 — @mention dispatch (@product for direct queries)
- #30 — Governance feed (PM observations surface here)
```

Perfect! I've now read all the files. Let me compile a comprehensive report with all the information you requested.

---

## Complete File Contents Summary

I have successfully read all 13 files you requested. Here is the complete information:

### 1. **Chat Component Catalog** (`app/src/client/chat-component-catalog.tsx`)

This file registers React components that render in chat messages:

- **EntityCard**: Displays extracted entities (projects, people, features, tasks, decisions, questions) with:
  - Kind label (Project, Person, Feature, etc.)
  - Category badge (if present)
  - Name and confidence score
  - Graph navigation button to view in graph view
  - Status badge

- **ExtractionSummary**: Container component showing:
  - Title
  - Grid of EntityCard components
  - Meta information about relationship count

Both components are exported to a `chatComponentCatalog` object created via `reachat`'s `componentCatalog()` helper.

### 2. **Chat Handler** (`app/src/server/chat/handler.ts`)

Main orchestrator entry point:
- **Function**: `runOrchestrator()` 
- Builds chat context from SurrealDB
- Creates system prompt
- Calls the AI model with tools
- Streams text tokens via `onToken` callback
- Uses `stepCountIs(5)` to limit tool calls
- Returns final assistant text

### 3. **Chat Processor** (`app/src/server/chat/chat-processor.ts`)

Main chat message processing workflow:

**Flow**:
1. Load workspace and conversation from database
2. Load conversation/graph context for extraction
3. If attachment: ingest and extract from document
4. Extract structured graph from user text
5. Persist extraction outputs (entities, relationships, embeddings)
6. Transition onboarding state
7. If onboarding complete: run orchestrator agent; else: generate onboarding reply
8. Build extraction component block for UI
9. Create assistant message record
10. Emit SSE events for: extraction, onboarding_seed, onboarding_state, assistant_message, done

**SSE Events Emitted**:
- `extraction`: Extracted entities and relationships
- `onboarding_seed`: Onboarding seed items
- `onboarding_state`: State transition
- `assistant_message`: Final response text and suggestions
- `token`: Streaming text tokens
- `done`: Completion signal
- `error`: Error notification

### 4. **Chat Context** (`app/src/server/chat/context.ts`)

Builds the context packet for the orchestrator agent:

**ChatContext Type**:
```typescript
{
  conversationEntities: ConversationEntity[]
  workspaceSummary: {
    projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[]
    recentDecisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[]
    openQuestions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[]
    openObservations: ObservationSummary[]
  }
}
```

**System Prompt Contents**:
- Orchestrator agent description
- Conversation entities already extracted
- Workspace overview (projects, decisions, questions, observations)
- Available tools list
- Subagent descriptions (invoke_pm_agent)
- UI component catalog reference
- Behavioral instructions (reference entities, check context first, use graph grounding, ask before confirm_decision)

### 5. **Chat Tools Index** (`app/src/server/chat/tools/index.ts`)

Registry of all orchestrator tools created via `createOrchestratorTools(deps)`:

**Available Tools**:
1. `search_entities` - Search workspace entities
2. `get_entity_detail` - Fetch full entity details
3. `get_project_status` - Project status aggregation
4. `get_conversation_history` - Load conversation messages
5. `resolve_decision` - Mark decision as resolved
6. `check_constraints` - Validate decision constraints
7. `create_provisional_decision` - Draft a decision
8. `confirm_decision` - Finalize a decision
9. `create_observation` - Create workspace observation
10. `acknowledge_observation` - Mark observation acknowledged
11. `resolve_observation` - Close an observation
12. `invoke_pm_agent` - Delegate to PM subagent

### 6. **Invoke PM Agent Tool** (`app/src/server/chat/tools/invoke-pm-agent.ts`)

Tool definition that delegates to PM subagent:

**Input Schema**:
- `intent`: enum of ["plan_work", "check_status", "organize", "track_dependencies"]
- `context`: Conversation context string
- `project`: Optional project scope

**Execution**: Calls `runPmAgent()` with orchestrator context and experimental_context

### 7. **Create Observation Tool** (`app/src/server/chat/tools/create-observation.ts`)

Creates observations in the workspace graph:

**Input Schema**:
- `text`: Observation text
- `severity`: "info" | "warning" | "conflict"
- `category`: Optional entity category
- `related_entity_id`: Optional entity reference (project/feature/task/decision/question)

**Execution Steps**:
1. Parse related entity ID if provided
2. Validate entity is in workspace scope
3. Create embedding vector
4. Call `createObservation()` query
5. Return observation ID and status

### 8. **PM Agent Prompt** (`app/src/server/agents/pm/prompt.ts`)

Builds system prompt for PM subagent:

**System Instructions**:
- PM is the authority on tasks, features, and project status
- All agent communication via knowledge graph
- Can create observations for risk/blockers
- Cannot create tasks/features directly (return suggestions)
- Use tools for missing data

**Key Rules**:
- Task dedup: exact match (>0.97 similarity) = duplicate_found
- Similar match (≥0.8 similarity) = possible_duplicate with similarity score
- Closed/completed items = new scope, keep as suggestion
- Status requests = call get_project_status
- Observations for cross-agent risks, conflicts, stale blockers

**Output**: Strict JSON schema with `summary`, `suggestions`, `updated`, `discarded`, `observations_created`

### 9. **PM Agent** (`app/src/server/agents/pm/agent.ts`)

Main PM subagent orchestrator:

**Types**:
- `WorkItemSuggestion`: task/feature with title, rationale, category, project, priority, possible_duplicate
- `PmAgentResult`: summary, suggestions[], updated[], discarded[], observations_created[]
- `PmAgentInput`: workspaceRecord, intent, context, project, etc.

**Execution**:
1. Build PM system prompt (loads projects and observations)
2. Set intent-specific instruction (plan_work vs check_status vs track_dependencies vs organize)
3. Call generateText with PM tools
4. Parse response as strict JSON
5. Validate against `pmAgentResultSchema`
6. Return parsed result

### 10. **PM Tools** (`app/src/server/agents/pm/tools.ts`)

PM subagent tool set (subset of orchestrator tools):

**Available to PM**:
1. `search_entities` - Search workspace
2. `get_project_status` - Project overview
3. `create_observation` - Log risks/blockers
4. `suggest_work_items` - Triage and deduplicate work items

### 11. **Suggest Work Items Tool** (`app/src/server/agents/pm/suggest-work-items.ts`)

Core deduplication logic for work items:

**Input**: Array of proposed work items (task or feature, with title, rationale, category, project, priority)

**For Each Item**:
1. Create embedding from title
2. Search for similar existing items (same kind)
3. Get top candidate by semantic similarity
4. **Decision Logic**:
   - No candidate found → add to `suggestions`
   - Candidate status is closed/completed → add to `suggestions` (new scope)
   - Score > 0.97 → add to `discarded` (exact duplicate)
   - Score ≥ 0.8 → add to `updated` (merge with existing)
   - Score < 0.8 → add to `suggestions` (new item)

**Output**: 
```typescript
{
  suggestions: SuggestedWorkItem[]
  updated: UpdatedWorkItem[]
  discarded: DiscardedWorkItem[]
}
```

---

## Key Architecture Observations

1. **Two-Tier Agent System**: 
   - Orchestrator (main agent) with broad tools
   - PM subagent (specialized for planning work) with focused tool set

2. **Graph-Based Communication**: Agents communicate through SurrealDB writes/reads, not text handoff

3. **Deduplication Strategy**: Uses semantic similarity thresholds (0.97 for exact, 0.8 for merge) on embeddings

4. **Component Rendering**: Chat includes structured component blocks (EntityCard, ExtractionSummary) embedded as markdown in assistant text

5. **SSE Event Streaming**: Real-time updates to client via multiple event types during processing

6. **Onboarding State Machine**: Workspace has onboarding phases (active → summary_pending → complete) that determine which agent handles response

7. **Extraction Persistence**: Entities, relationships, and embeddings are written to graph during message processing, not at display time

All files read completely as requested.

    24→      <circle cx="18" cy="6" r="3" />

    32→function EntityCard(props: EntityCardProps) {

    56→              <GraphLinkIcon />

    58→          ) : undefined}
    59→          <span className="entity-status">{props.status}</span>
    60→        </div>
    61→      </div>
    62→      <p className="entity-name">{props.name}</p>
    63→      <p className="entity-confidence">Confidence {props.confidence.toFixed(2)}</p>
    64→    </article>
    65→  );
    66→}
    67→

     1→import { stepCountIs, streamText, type ModelMessage } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { buildChatContext, buildSystemPrompt } from "./context";
     4→import { createOrchestratorTools } from "./tools";
     5→
     6→type ConversationMessage = {
     7→  role: "user" | "assistant";
     8→  text: string;
     9→};
    10→
    11→export async function runOrchestrator(input: {
    12→  surreal: Surreal;
    13→  model: any;
    14→  pmModel: any;
    15→  embeddingModel: any;
    16→  embeddingDimension: number;
    17→  extractionModelId: string;
    18→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    19→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    20→  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    21→  latestUserText: string;
    22→  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
    23→  messages: ConversationMessage[];
    24→  onToken: (token: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    25→}): Promise<{ text: string }> {
    26→  const context = await buildChatContext({
    27→    surreal: input.surreal,
    28→    conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    29→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    30→  });
    31→
    32→  const system = buildSystemPrompt(context);
    33→  const modelMessages: ModelMessage[] = input.messages.map((message) => ({
    34→    role: message.role,
    35→    content: message.text,
    36→  }));
    37→
    38→  const result = streamText({
    39→    model: input.model,
    40→    system,
    41→    messages: modelMessages,
    42→    tools: createOrchestratorTools({
    43→      surreal: input.surreal,
    44→      pmModel: input.pmModel,
    45→      embeddingModel: input.embeddingModel,
    46→      embeddingDimension: input.embeddingDimension,
    47→      extractionModelId: input.extractionModelId,
    48→    }),
    49→    experimental_context: {
    50→      actor: "orchestrator",
    51→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    52→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    53→      currentMessageRecord: input.currentMessageRecord,
    54→      latestUserText: input.latestUserText,
    55→      ...(input.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord: input.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
    56→    },
    57→    stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
    58→  });
    59→
    60→  let text = "";
    61→  for await (const part of result.fullStream) {
    62→    if (part.type !== "text-delta") {
    63→      continue;
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    text = `${text}${part.text}`;
    67→    await input.onToken(part.text);
    68→  }
    69→
    70→  if (text.trim().length === 0) {
    71→    text = await result.text;
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  return {
    75→    text: text.trim(),
    76→  };
    77→}
    78→
    79→export const runGraphAwareChat = runOrchestrator;
    80→

     1→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { ObservationSummary } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import {
     4→  listConversationEntities,
     5→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     6→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     7→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     8→  type ConversationEntity,
     9→  type WorkspaceDecisionSummary,
    10→  type WorkspaceProjectSummary,
    11→  type WorkspaceQuestionSummary,
    12→} from "../graph/queries";
    13→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    14→import { chatComponentSystemPrompt } from "./chat-component-system-prompt";
    15→
    16→export type ChatContext = {
    17→  conversationEntities: ConversationEntity[];
    18→  workspaceSummary: {
    19→    projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[];
    20→    recentDecisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[];
    21→    openQuestions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[];
    22→    openObservations: ObservationSummary[];
    23→  };
    24→};
    25→
    26→type ChatContextLoaders = {
    27→  listConversationEntities: typeof listConversationEntities;
    28→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries: typeof listWorkspaceProjectSummaries;
    29→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions: typeof listWorkspaceRecentDecisions;
    30→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions: typeof listWorkspaceOpenQuestions;
    31→  listWorkspaceOpenObservations: typeof listWorkspaceOpenObservations;
    32→};
    33→
    34→export async function buildChatContext(input: {
    35→  surreal: Surreal;
    36→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    37→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    38→  loaders?: ChatContextLoaders;
    39→}): Promise<ChatContext> {
    40→  const loaders = input.loaders ?? {
    41→    listConversationEntities,
    42→    listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
    43→    listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
    44→    listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
    45→    listWorkspaceOpenObservations,
    46→  };
    47→
    48→  const [conversationEntities, projects, recentDecisions, openQuestions, openObservations] = await Promise.all([
    49→    loaders.listConversationEntities({
    50→      surreal: input.surreal,
    51→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    52→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    53→      limit: 60,
    54→    }),
    55→    loaders.listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    56→      surreal: input.surreal,
    57→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    58→      limit: 20,
    59→    }),
    60→    loaders.listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({
    61→      surreal: input.surreal,
    62→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    63→      limit: 12,
    64→    }),
    65→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({
    66→      surreal: input.surreal,
    67→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    68→      limit: 12,
    69→    }),
    70→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
    71→      surreal: input.surreal,
    72→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    73→      limit: 10,
    74→    }),
    75→  ]);
    76→
    77→  return {
    78→    conversationEntities,
    79→    workspaceSummary: {
    80→      projects,
    81→      recentDecisions,
    82→      openQuestions,
    83→      openObservations,
    84→    },
    85→  };
    86→}
    87→
    88→function formatConversationEntities(entities: ConversationEntity[]): string {
    89→  if (entities.length === 0) {
    90→    return "- none";
    91→  }
    92→
    93→  return entities
    94→    .slice(0, 20)
    95→    .map((entity) => `- ${entity.kind}: ${entity.name} (confidence ${entity.confidence.toFixed(2)})`)
    96→    .join("\n");
    97→}
    98→
    99→function formatProjectList(projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[]): string {
   100→  if (projects.length === 0) {
   101→    return "- none";
   102→  }
   103→
   104→  return projects
   105→    .slice(0, 15)
   106→    .map((project) => `- ${project.name} [id: ${project.id}] active tasks: ${project.activeTaskCount}`)
   107→    .join("\n");
   108→}
   109→
   110→function formatDecisionList(decisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[]): string {
   111→  if (decisions.length === 0) {
   112→    return "- none";
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  return decisions
   116→    .slice(0, 15)
   117→    .map((decision) => {
   118→      const project = decision.project ? ` project: ${decision.project}` : "";
   119→      return `- ${decision.name} [id: ${decision.id}] status: ${decision.status}${project}`;
   120→    })
   121→    .join("\n");
   122→}
   123→
   124→function formatQuestionList(questions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[]): string {
   125→  if (questions.length === 0) {
   126→    return "- none";
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  return questions
   130→    .slice(0, 15)
   131→    .map((question) => {
   132→      const project = question.project ? ` project: ${question.project}` : "";
   133→      return `- ${question.name} [id: ${question.id}]${project}`;
   134→    })
   135→    .join("\n");
   136→}
   137→
   138→function formatObservationList(observations: ObservationSummary[]): string {
   139→  if (observations.length === 0) {
   140→    return "- none";
   141→  }
   142→
   143→  return observations
   144→    .slice(0, 10)
   145→    .map((observation) => {
   146→      const category = observation.category ? `, ${observation.category}` : "";
   147→      return `- [${observation.severity}] ${observation.text} (by ${observation.sourceAgent}, ${observation.status}${category})`;
   148→    })
   149→    .join("\n");
   150→}
   151→
   152→export function buildSystemPrompt(context: ChatContext): string {
   153→  return [
   154→    "You are the Orchestrator agent for a workspace-aware project intelligence system.",
   155→    "Route complex requests to specialized tools and subagents when they can do better than direct response.",
   156→    "You have access to a workspace-scoped knowledge graph stored in SurrealDB.",
   157→    "Never assume data from other workspaces.",
   158→    "",
   159→    "## This Conversation",
   160→    "Entities already extracted from this conversation:",
   161→    formatConversationEntities(context.conversationEntities),
   162→    "",
   163→    "## Workspace Overview",
   164→    "Projects:",
   165→    formatProjectList(context.workspaceSummary.projects),
   166→    "",
   167→    "Recent decisions:",
   168→    formatDecisionList(context.workspaceSummary.recentDecisions),
   169→    "",
   170→    "Open questions:",
   171→    formatQuestionList(context.workspaceSummary.openQuestions),
   172→    "",
   173→    "## Active Observations",
   174→    formatObservationList(context.workspaceSummary.openObservations),
   175→    "",
   176→    "## Tools",
   177→    "Use tools for anything that needs deeper lookup, graph traversal, provenance, or decision actions.",
   178→    "Use `invoke_pm_agent` for planning work, dependency tracking, and project organization requests.",
   179→    "",
   180→    "## Subagents",
   181→    "- invoke_pm_agent: Product Manager. Use for work planning, project status checks, organization, and dependency tracking.",
   182→    "- The PM agent checks for duplicates before suggesting work items and can create observations.",
   183→    "- Do not hand off raw tool output between agents. Agents communicate through graph writes and follow-up graph reads.",
   184→    "",
   185→    "## UI Components",
   186→    "When useful, render structured component blocks that match the available catalog.",
   187→    chatComponentSystemPrompt,
   188→    "",
   189→    "## Behavior",
   190→    "- Reference entities by name when relevant.",
   191→    "- Check context first, then use tools when needed.",
   192→    "- Act as a thin orchestrator: decide tool/subagent sequencing, then synthesize from graph-grounded results.",

   194→    "- Ask for explicit confirmation before calling confirm_decision.",

    17→import { loadWorkspaceProjects } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    18→
    19→export async function processChatMessage(input: {
    20→  deps: ServerDependencies;
    21→  conversationId: string;
    22→  messageId: string;
    23→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    24→  userMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    25→  userText: string;
    26→  attachment?: IncomingAttachment;
    27→  onboardingAction?: OnboardingAction;
    28→}): Promise<void> {
    29→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    30→  logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.started", "Chat message processing execution started", {
    31→    conversationId: input.conversationId,
    32→    messageId: input.messageId,
    33→    workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
    34→    hasAttachment: input.attachment !== undefined,
    35→  });
    36→
    37→  try {
    38→    const now = new Date();
    39→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", input.conversationId);
    40→    const workspace = await input.deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(input.workspaceRecord);
    41→    if (!workspace) {
    42→      throw new Error("workspace not found");
    43→    }
    44→
    45→    const conversation = await input.deps.surreal.select<ConversationRow>(conversationRecord);
    46→    if (!conversation) {
    47→      throw new Error("conversation not found");
    48→    }
    49→
    50→    const assistantContextRows = await loadAssistantConversationContext(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId);
    51→    const extractionConversationContext = await loadExtractionConversationContext({
    52→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    53→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
    54→      currentMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
    55→    });
    56→    const extractionGraphContext = await loadConversationGraphContext(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId, 60);
    57→    const workspaceProjects = await loadWorkspaceProjects(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord);
    58→    const workspaceProjectNames = workspaceProjects.map((project) => project.name);
    59→    const persistedEntities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
    60→    const persistedRelationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
    61→    const seedItems: OnboardingSeedItem[] = [];
    62→    const embeddingTargets: Array<{ record: GraphEntityRecord; text: string }> = [];
    63→    const extractedTools: string[] = [];
    64→    const unresolvedAssigneeNames = new Set<string>();
    65→
    66→    if (input.attachment) {
    67→      const ingestion = await ingestAttachment({
    68→        surreal: input.deps.surreal,
    69→        extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
    70→        embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
    71→        embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
    72→        extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
    73→        extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
    74→        workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    75→        conversationRecord,
    76→        userMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
    77→        attachment: input.attachment,
    78→        workspaceName: workspace.name,
    79→        projectNames: workspaceProjectNames,
    80→        now,
    81→        onChunkResult: (chunkResult) => {
    82→          if (chunkResult.entities.length > 0 || chunkResult.relationships.length > 0) {
    83→            input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
    84→              type: "extraction",
    85→              messageId: input.messageId,
    86→              entities: chunkResult.entities,
    87→              relationships: chunkResult.relationships,
    88→            });
    89→          }
    90→          if (chunkResult.seeds.length > 0) {
    91→            input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
    92→              type: "onboarding_seed",
    93→              messageId: input.messageId,
    94→              seeds: chunkResult.seeds,
    95→            });
    96→          }
    97→        },
    98→      });
    99→
   100→      persistedEntities.push(...ingestion.entities);
   101→      persistedRelationships.push(...ingestion.relationships);
   102→      seedItems.push(...ingestion.seeds);
   103→      embeddingTargets.push(...ingestion.embeddingTargets);
   104→      extractedTools.push(...ingestion.tools);
   105→      for (const unresolvedName of ingestion.unresolvedAssigneeNames) {
   106→        unresolvedAssigneeNames.add(unresolvedName);
   107→      }
   108→    }
   109→
   110→    const textExtraction = await extractStructuredGraph({
   111→      extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
   112→      conversationHistory: extractionConversationContext.conversationHistory,
   113→      currentMessage: extractionConversationContext.currentMessage,
   114→      graphContext: extractionGraphContext,
   115→      sourceText: input.userText,
   116→      onboarding: !workspace.onboarding_complete,
   117→      workspaceName: workspace.name,
   118→      projectNames: workspaceProjectNames,
   119→    });
   120→
   121→    const textPersistence = await persistExtractionOutput({
   122→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   123→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   124→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   125→      extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   126→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   127→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   128→      sourceRecord: input.userMessageRecord as SourceRecord,
   129→      sourceKind: "message",
   130→      sourceLabel: input.userText.slice(0, 140),
   131→      promptText: input.userText,
   132→      output: textExtraction,
   133→      sourceMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   134→      extractionHistoryMessageIds: extractionConversationContext.conversationHistory.map((row) => row.id.id as string),
   135→      now,
   136→    });
   137→
   138→    persistedEntities.push(...textPersistence.entities);
   139→    persistedRelationships.push(...textPersistence.relationships);
   140→    seedItems.push(...textPersistence.seeds);
   141→    embeddingTargets.push(...textPersistence.embeddingTargets);
   142→    extractedTools.push(...textPersistence.tools);
   143→    for (const unresolvedName of textPersistence.unresolvedAssigneeNames) {
   144→      unresolvedAssigneeNames.add(unresolvedName);
   145→    }
   146→
   147→    const dedupedTools = [...new Set(extractedTools.map((tool) => tool.trim()).filter((tool) => tool.length > 0))];
   148→    await appendExtractedTools(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, extractedTools, now);
   149→
   150→    await refreshConversationTouchedBy(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   151→    await maybeUpgradeConversationTitle(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   152→
   153→    const onboardingBefore = workspace.onboarding_complete
   154→      ? "complete"
   155→      : workspace.onboarding_summary_pending
   156→        ? "summary_pending"
   157→        : "active";
   158→
   159→    const onboardingAfter = await transitionOnboardingState({
   160→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   161→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   162→      workspace,
   163→      onboardingAction: input.onboardingAction,
   164→      now,
   165→    });
   166→
   167→    let assistantText = "";
   168→    let assistantSuggestions: string[] = [];
   169→    const unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions = [...unresolvedAssigneeNames].map(
   170→      (name) => `You mentioned ${name} - want to add them to workspace people?`,
   171→    );
   172→
   173→    if (onboardingAfter === "complete") {
   174→      const workspaceOwnerRecord = await getWorkspaceOwnerRecord({
   175→        surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   176→        workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   177→      });
   178→
   179→      const graphAwareResponse = await runOrchestrator({
   180→        surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   181→        model: input.deps.assistantModel,
   182→        pmModel: input.deps.pmModel,
   183→        embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   184→        embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   185→        extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   186→        conversationRecord,
   187→        workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   188→        currentMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   189→        latestUserText: input.userText,
   190→        ...(workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
   191→        messages: assistantContextRows.map((row) => ({
   192→          role: row.role,
   193→          text: row.text,
   194→        })),
   195→        onToken: async (token) => {
   196→          input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   197→            type: "token",
   198→            messageId: input.messageId,
   199→            token,
   200→          });
   201→        },
   202→      });
   203→
   204→      assistantText = graphAwareResponse.text.trim();
   205→    } else {
   206→      const assistantReply = await generateOnboardingAssistantReply({
   207→        assistantModel: input.deps.assistantModel,
   208→        surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   209→        onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   210→        contextRows: assistantContextRows,
   211→        latestUserText: input.userText,
   212→        workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   213→        latestEntities: persistedEntities.map((entity) => ({
   214→          kind: entity.kind,
   215→          text: entity.text,
   216→          confidence: entity.confidence,
   217→        })),
   218→        latestTools: dedupedTools,
   219→      });
   220→
   221→      assistantText = assistantReply.message.trim();
   222→      assistantSuggestions = assistantReply.suggestions;
   223→
   224→      for (const token of assistantText.split(" ")) {
   225→        input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   226→          type: "token",
   227→          messageId: input.messageId,
   228→          token: `${token} `,
   229→        });
   230→        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 25));
   231→      }
   232→    }
   233→
   234→    assistantSuggestions = sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(
   235→      [...assistantSuggestions, ...unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions],
   236→      3,
   237→    );
   238→
   239→    const summaryBlock = buildExtractionComponentBlock(
   240→      persistedEntities,
   241→      persistedRelationships,
   242→      input.deps.config.extractionDisplayThreshold,
   243→    );
   244→
   245→    if (summaryBlock) {
   246→      if (onboardingAfter === "complete") {
   247→        const streamChunk = assistantText.length > 0 ? `\n\n${summaryBlock}` : summaryBlock;
   248→        for (const token of streamChunk.split(" ")) {
   249→          input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   250→            type: "token",
   251→            messageId: input.messageId,
   252→            token: `${token} `,
   253→          });
   254→        }
   255→      }
   256→
   257→      assistantText = assistantText.length > 0 ? `${assistantText}\n\n${summaryBlock}` : summaryBlock;
   258→    }
   259→
   260→    if (assistantText.trim().length === 0) {
   261→      assistantText = "I could not generate a response for that request.";
   262→    }
   263→
   264→    const assistantMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", input.messageId);
   265→    await input.deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
   266→      conversation: conversationRecord,
   267→      role: "assistant",
   268→      text: assistantText,
   269→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   270→      createdAt: now,
   271→    });
   272→
   273→    await input.deps.surreal.update(conversationRecord).merge({
   274→      updatedAt: now,
   275→    });
   276→
   277→    void persistEmbeddings({
   278→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   279→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   280→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   281→      assistantMessageRecord,
   282→      assistantText,
   283→      entities: embeddingTargets,
   284→    }).catch(() => undefined);
   285→
   286→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   287→      type: "extraction",
   288→      messageId: input.messageId,
   289→      entities: persistedEntities,
   290→      relationships: persistedRelationships,
   291→    });
   292→
   293→    if (seedItems.length > 0) {
   294→      input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   295→        type: "onboarding_seed",
   296→        messageId: input.messageId,
   297→        seeds: seedItems,
   298→      });
   299→    }
   300→
   301→    if (onboardingBefore !== onboardingAfter) {
   302→      input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   303→        type: "onboarding_state",
   304→        messageId: input.messageId,
   305→        onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   306→      });
   307→    }
   308→
   309→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   310→      type: "assistant_message",
   311→      messageId: input.messageId,
   312→      text: assistantText,
   313→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   314→    });
   315→
   316→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   317→      type: "done",
   318→      messageId: input.messageId,
   319→    });
   320→
   321→    logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.completed", "Chat message processing execution completed", {
   322→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   323→      messageId: input.messageId,
   324→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   325→      entityCount: persistedEntities.length,
   326→      relationshipCount: persistedRelationships.length,
   327→      seedCount: seedItems.length,
   328→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   329→    });
   330→  } catch (error) {
   331→    logError("chat.message.process.execution.failed", "Chat message processing execution failed", error, {
   332→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   333→      messageId: input.messageId,
   334→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   335→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   336→    });
   337→    const errorText = userFacingError(error, "chat processing failed");
   338→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   339→      type: "error",
   340→      messageId: input.messageId,
   341→      error: errorText,
   342→    });
   343→  }
   344→}
   345→
   346→function sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(suggestions: string[], limit: number): string[] {
   347→  return [...new Set(suggestions.map((value) => value.trim()))]
   348→    .filter((value) => value.length > 0 && value.length <= 140)
   349→    .slice(0, limit);
   350→}
   351→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { runPmAgent } from "../../agents/pm/agent";
     4→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     5→import type { OrchestratorToolDeps } from "./types";
     6→
     7→export function createInvokePmAgentTool(deps: OrchestratorToolDeps) {
     8→  return tool({
     9→    description:
    10→      "Invoke the PM subagent for planning work, checking project status, organizing initiatives, and tracking dependencies.",
    11→    inputSchema: z.object({
    12→      intent: z.enum(["plan_work", "check_status", "organize", "track_dependencies"]),
    13→      context: z.string().min(1).describe("Conversation context for the PM agent"),
    14→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    15→    }),
    16→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    17→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    18→
    19→      return runPmAgent({
    20→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    21→        pmModel: deps.pmModel,
    22→        embeddingModel: deps.embeddingModel,
    23→        embeddingDimension: deps.embeddingDimension,
    24→        extractionModelId: deps.extractionModelId,
    25→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    26→        conversationRecord: context.conversationRecord,
    27→        currentMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    28→        latestUserText: context.latestUserText,
    29→        intent: input.intent,
    30→        context: input.context,
    31→        ...(input.project ? { project: input.project } : {}),
    32→        ...(context.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord: context.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
    33→      });
    34→    },
    35→  });
    36→}
    37→

     1→import { generateText } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, type EntityCategory } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     5→import { buildPmSystemPrompt } from "./prompt";
     6→import { createPmTools } from "./tools";
     7→
     8→const workItemSuggestionSchema = z.object({
     9→  kind: z.enum(["task", "feature"]),
    10→  title: z.string().min(1),
    11→  rationale: z.string().min(1),
    12→  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),
    13→  project: z.string().optional(),
    14→  priority: z.string().optional(),
    15→  possible_duplicate: z
    16→    .object({
    17→      id: z.string().min(1),
    18→      name: z.string().min(1),
    19→      similarity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    20→    })
    21→    .optional(),
    22→});
    23→
    24→const pmAgentResultSchema = z.object({
    25→  summary: z.string().min(1),
    26→  suggestions: z.array(workItemSuggestionSchema),
    27→  updated: z.array(
    28→    z.object({
    29→      existing_id: z.string().min(1),
    30→      title: z.string().min(1),
    31→      changes: z.string().min(1),
    32→    }),
    33→  ),
    34→  discarded: z.array(
    35→    z.object({
    36→      title: z.string().min(1),
    37→      reason: z.string().min(1),
    38→    }),
    39→  ),
    40→  observations_created: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    41→});
    42→
    43→export type WorkItemSuggestion = {
    44→  kind: "task" | "feature";
    45→  title: string;
    46→  rationale: string;
    47→  category?: EntityCategory;
    48→  project?: string;
    49→  priority?: string;
    50→  possible_duplicate?: {
    51→    id: string;
    52→    name: string;
    53→    similarity: number;
    54→  };
    55→};
    56→
    57→export type PmAgentResult = {
    58→  summary: string;
    59→  suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[];
    60→  updated: Array<{ existing_id: string; title: string; changes: string }>;
    61→  discarded: Array<{ title: string; reason: string }>;
    62→  observations_created: string[];
    63→};
    64→
    65→export type PmAgentInput = {
    66→  surreal: Surreal;
    67→  pmModel: any;
    68→  embeddingModel: any;
    69→  embeddingDimension: number;
    70→  extractionModelId: string;
    71→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    72→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    73→  currentMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    74→  latestUserText: string;
    75→  intent: "plan_work" | "check_status" | "organize" | "track_dependencies";
    76→  context: string;
    77→  project?: string;
    78→  workspaceOwnerRecord?: RecordId<"person", string>;
    79→};
    80→
    81→function parseJsonPayload(raw: string): unknown {
    82→  const trimmed = raw.trim();
    83→  if (trimmed.length === 0) {
    84→    throw new Error("pm agent returned empty text");
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  try {
    88→    return JSON.parse(trimmed);
    89→  } catch (error) {
    90→    throw new Error(`pm agent output is not valid strict JSON: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "parse error"}`);
    91→  }
    92→}
    93→
    94→export async function runPmAgent(input: PmAgentInput): Promise<PmAgentResult> {
    95→  const system = await buildPmSystemPrompt({
    96→    surreal: input.surreal,
    97→    workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    98→  });
    99→  const intentInstruction = input.intent === "check_status"
   100→    ? "Primary action: call get_project_status when a project scope is available."
   101→    : input.intent === "plan_work"
   102→      ? "Primary action: propose tasks/features with suggest_work_items, deduping each item."
   103→      : input.intent === "track_dependencies"
   104→        ? "Primary action: identify blockers/dependencies and create observations for high-risk paths."
   105→        : "Primary action: organize work into clear, deduplicated next steps.";
   106→
   107→  const result = await generateText({
   108→    model: input.pmModel,
   109→    system,
   110→    prompt: [
   111→      "You are handling a PM request.",
   112→      `Intent: ${input.intent}`,
   113→      input.project ? `Project hint: ${input.project}` : "Project hint: not provided",
   114→      intentInstruction,
   115→      "Context:",
   116→      input.context,
   117→      "",
   118→      "Return strict JSON only. Do not include markdown.",
   119→      "Schema:",
   120→      '{"summary": string, "suggestions": WorkItemSuggestion[], "updated": Array<{"existing_id": string, "title": string, "changes": string}>, "discarded": Array<{"title": string, "reason": string}>, "observations_created": string[]}',
   121→    ].join("\n"),
   122→    tools: createPmTools({
   123→      surreal: input.surreal,
   124→      embeddingModel: input.embeddingModel,
   125→      embeddingDimension: input.embeddingDimension,
   126→      extractionModelId: input.extractionModelId,
   127→    }),
   128→    experimental_context: {
   129→      actor: "pm_agent",
   130→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   131→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
   132→      currentMessageRecord: input.currentMessageRecord,
   133→      latestUserText: input.latestUserText,
   134→      ...(input.workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord: input.workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
   135→    },
   136→    maxSteps: 5,
   137→  });
   138→
   139→  const parsed = pmAgentResultSchema.safeParse(parseJsonPayload(result.text));
   140→  if (!parsed.success) {
   141→    throw new Error(`pm agent output failed validation: ${parsed.error.message}`);
   142→  }
   143→
   144→  return parsed.data;
   145→}
   146→

     1→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import { listWorkspaceProjectSummaries } from "../../graph/queries";
     3→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../../observation/queries";
     4→
     5→function formatProjects(rows: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listWorkspaceProjectSummaries>>): string {
     6→  if (rows.length === 0) {
     7→    return "- none";
     8→  }
     9→
    10→  return rows
    11→    .slice(0, 20)
    12→    .map((row) => `- ${row.name} [id: ${row.id}] active tasks: ${row.activeTaskCount}`)
    13→    .join("\n");
    14→}
    15→
    16→function formatObservations(rows: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listWorkspaceOpenObservations>>): string {
    17→  if (rows.length === 0) {
    18→    return "- none";
    19→  }
    20→
    21→  return rows
    22→    .slice(0, 20)
    23→    .map((row) => {
    24→      const category = row.category ? `, ${row.category}` : "";
    25→      return `- [${row.severity}] ${row.text} (${row.status}, by ${row.sourceAgent}${category})`;
    26→    })
    27→    .join("\n");
    28→}
    29→
    30→export async function buildPmSystemPrompt(input: {
    31→  surreal: Surreal;
    32→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    33→}): Promise<string> {
    34→  const [projects, observations] = await Promise.all([
    35→    listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    36→      surreal: input.surreal,
    37→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    38→      limit: 20,
    39→    }),
    40→    listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
    41→      surreal: input.surreal,
    42→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    43→      limit: 20,
    44→    }),
    45→  ]);
    46→
    47→  return [
    48→    "You are the Product Manager subagent.",
    49→    "You are the single authority on tasks, features, and project status.",
    50→    "All agent-to-agent communication happens through the knowledge graph.",
    51→    "Never rely on handoff text from other agents; read context from graph tools.",
    52→    "You may create observations directly when you detect risk, blockers, or conflicts.",
    53→    "Do not create tasks or features directly in this workflow; return suggestions for user approval.",
    54→    "Use tools whenever relevant data is missing.",
    55→    "",
    56→    "## Workspace Projects",
    57→    formatProjects(projects),
    58→    "",
    59→    "## Active Observations",
    60→    formatObservations(observations),
    61→    "",
    62→    "## Core Responsibilities",
    63→    "- Task dedup and merge awareness across all agent suggestions.",
    64→    "- Feature and dependency risk tracking (surface blockers via observations).",
    65→    "- Project status aggregation when the user asks for status, progress, or blockers.",
    66→    "",
    67→    "## Task Suggestion Rules",
    68→    "1. Use suggest_work_items for every proposed task or feature.",
    69→    "2. Exact duplicate of an open/in_progress item: treat as duplicate_found.",
    70→    "3. Similar open/in_progress item with meaningful scope overlap: include possible_duplicate.",
    71→    "4. Similar closed/completed item: treat as new scope and keep as suggestion.",
    72→    "5. No meaningful match: keep as new suggestion.",
    73→    "",
    74→    "## Project Status Rules",
    75→    "- When intent is status/progress/blockers, call get_project_status.",
    76→    "- Include active tasks, blocked tasks, recent decisions, open questions, and active observations.",
    77→    "",
    78→    "## Observation Rules",
    79→    "- Create observation entries for cross-agent risks, conflicts, stale blockers, and missing execution paths.",
    80→    "- Set severity to conflict/warning/info based on urgency.",
    81→    "",
    82→    "## Output",
    83→    "Return strict JSON only (no markdown).",
    84→    "Match this schema exactly: summary, suggestions, updated, discarded, observations_created.",
    85→    "If no suggestions remain after dedup, return suggestions: [].",
    86→  ].join("\n");
    87→}
    88→

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, type EntityCategory } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     5→import { resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord, searchEntitiesByEmbedding } from "../../graph/queries";
     6→import { requireToolContext } from "../../chat/tools/helpers";
     7→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "../../chat/tools/types";
     8→
     9→type SuggestedWorkItem = {
    10→  kind: "task" | "feature";
    11→  title: string;
    12→  rationale: string;
    13→  category?: EntityCategory;
    14→  project?: string;
    15→  priority?: string;
    16→  possible_duplicate?: {
    17→    id: string;
    18→    name: string;
    19→    similarity: number;
    20→  };
    21→};
    22→
    23→type UpdatedWorkItem = {
    24→  existing_id: string;
    25→  title: string;
    26→  changes: string;
    27→};
    28→
    29→type DiscardedWorkItem = {
    30→  title: string;
    31→  reason: string;
    32→};
    33→
    34→const CLOSED_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "completed", "closed", "resolved", "superseded"]);
    35→
    36→export function createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    37→  return tool({
    38→    description:
    39→      "Process a batch of proposed work items and return issue-style PM triage buckets: suggestions, updated, discarded.",
    40→    inputSchema: z.object({
    41→      items: z.array(
    42→        z.object({
    43→          kind: z.enum(["task", "feature"]).describe("Work item kind"),
    44→          title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise work item title"),
    45→          rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this work item is needed"),
    46→          category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional work item category"),
    47→          project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    48→          priority: z.string().optional().describe("Optional priority hint"),
    49→        }),
    50→      ).min(1).max(25),
    51→    }),
    52→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    53→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    54→      const suggestions: SuggestedWorkItem[] = [];
    55→      const updated: UpdatedWorkItem[] = [];
    56→      const discarded: DiscardedWorkItem[] = [];
    57→
    58→      for (const item of input.items) {
    59→        const suggestion: SuggestedWorkItem = {
    60→          kind: item.kind,
    61→          title: item.title,
    62→          rationale: item.rationale,
    63→          ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
    64→          ...(item.project ? { project: item.project } : {}),
    65→          ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),
    66→        };
    67→
    68→        const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(deps.embeddingModel, item.title, deps.embeddingDimension);
    69→        if (!embedding) {
    70→          throw new Error("failed to create embedding for suggest_work_items");
    71→        }
    72→
    73→        const projectRecord = item.project
    74→          ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    75→              surreal: deps.surreal,
    76→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    77→              projectInput: item.project,
    78→            })
    79→          : undefined;
    80→
    81→        const candidates = await searchEntitiesByEmbedding({
    82→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    83→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    84→          queryEmbedding: embedding,
    85→          kinds: [item.kind],
    86→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    87→          limit: 5,
    88→        });
    89→
    90→        const top = candidates[0];
    91→        if (!top) {
    92→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    93→          continue;
    94→        }
    95→
    96→        const normalizedTopStatus = top.status ? top.status.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;
    97→        if (normalizedTopStatus && CLOSED_STATUSES.has(normalizedTopStatus)) {
    98→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    99→          continue;
   100→        }
   101→
   102→        if (top.score > 0.97) {
   103→          discarded.push({
   104→            title: item.title,
   105→            reason: `Exact duplicate of existing item ${top.kind}:${top.id} (${top.name})`,
   106→          });
   107→          continue;
   108→        }
   109→
   110→        if (top.score >= 0.8) {
   111→          updated.push({
   112→            existing_id: `${top.kind}:${top.id}`,
   113→            title: top.name,
   114→            changes: `Merge context from suggested item '${item.title}'`,
   115→          });
   116→          continue;
   117→        }
   118→
   119→        suggestions.push(suggestion);
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      return {
   123→        suggestions,
   124→        updated,
   125→        discarded,
   126→      };
   127→    },
   128→  });
   129→}
   130→
```

```
     1→import type { ToolExecutionOptions } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import type { ChatToolExecutionContext } from "./types";
     4→
     5→export function requireToolContext(options: ToolExecutionOptions): ChatToolExecutionContext {
     6→  const context = options.experimental_context as ChatToolExecutionContext | undefined;
     7→  if (!context) {
     8→    throw new Error("chat tool execution context is missing");
     9→  }
    10→
    11→  if (!context.workspaceRecord || !context.currentMessageRecord || !context.conversationRecord) {
    12→    throw new Error("chat tool execution context is malformed");
    13→  }
    14→
    15→  return context;
    16→}
    17→
    18→export function toDecisionRecordId(value: string): RecordId<"decision", string> {
    19→  const normalized = value.trim();
    20→  if (normalized.length === 0) {
    21→    throw new Error("decision id is required");
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  if (normalized.includes(":")) {
    25→    const [table, id] = normalized.split(":", 2);
    26→    if (table !== "decision" || !id) {
    27→      throw new Error("decision id must reference a decision record");
    28→    }
    29→    return new RecordId("decision", id);
    30→  }
    31→
    32→  return new RecordId("decision", normalized);
    33→}
    34→
```

     1→import { create } from "zustand";
     2→
     3→type ViewState = {
     4→  selectedEntityId: string | undefined;
     5→  graphViewMode: "project" | "focused";
     6→  graphProjectId: string | undefined;
     7→  graphCenterId: string | undefined;
     8→  graphDepth: number;
     9→  highlightMessageId: string | undefined;
    10→
    11→  selectEntity: (entityId: string | undefined) => void;
    12→  setGraphViewMode: (mode: "project" | "focused") => void;
    13→  setGraphProject: (projectId: string) => void;
    14→  setGraphDepth: (depth: number) => void;
    15→  navigateToGraph: (entityId: string) => void;
    16→  navigateToChat: (messageId: string) => void;
    17→  clearHighlight: () => void;
    18→};
    19→
    20→export const useViewState = create<ViewState>((set) => ({
    21→  selectedEntityId: undefined,
    22→  graphViewMode: "project",
    23→  graphProjectId: undefined,
    24→  graphCenterId: undefined,
    25→  graphDepth: 2,
    26→  highlightMessageId: undefined,
    27→
    28→  selectEntity: (entityId) => set({ selectedEntityId: entityId }),
    29→
    30→  setGraphViewMode: (mode) => set({ graphViewMode: mode }),
    31→
    32→  setGraphProject: (projectId) =>
    33→    set({ graphProjectId: projectId, graphViewMode: "project" }),
    34→
    35→  setGraphDepth: (depth) => set({ graphDepth: Math.max(1, Math.min(3, depth)) }),
    36→
    37→  navigateToGraph: (entityId) =>
    38→    set({
    39→      graphCenterId: entityId,
    40→      graphViewMode: "focused",
    41→      selectedEntityId: entityId,
    42→    }),
    43→
    44→  navigateToChat: (messageId) =>
    45→    set({ highlightMessageId: messageId }),
    46→
    47→  clearHighlight: () => set({ highlightMessageId: undefined }),
    48→}));
    49→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     5→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     6→import { isEntityInWorkspace, parseRecordIdString } from "../../graph/queries";
     7→import { createObservation } from "../../observation/queries";
     8→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     9→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    10→
    11→export function createCreateObservationTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    12→  return tool({
    13→    description:
    14→      "Create an observation in the workspace graph. Use this for warnings, conflicts, or notable facts that should be tracked.",
    15→    inputSchema: z.object({
    16→      text: z.string().min(1).describe("Observation text"),
    17→      severity: z.enum(["info", "warning", "conflict"]).describe("Observation severity"),
    18→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional observation category"),
    19→      related_entity_id: z
    20→        .string()
    21→        .optional()
    22→        .describe("Optional related entity id (project/feature/task/decision/question)"),
    23→    }),
    24→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    25→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    26→
    27→      const relatedRecord = input.related_entity_id
    28→        ? parseRecordIdString(
    29→            input.related_entity_id,
    30→            ["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"],
    31→          ) as RecordId<"project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question", string>
    32→        : undefined;
    33→
    34→      if (relatedRecord) {
    35→        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, relatedRecord);
    36→        if (!scoped) {
    37→          throw new Error("related entity is outside the current workspace scope");
    38→        }
    39→      }
    40→
    41→      const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(deps.embeddingModel, input.text, deps.embeddingDimension);
    42→      if (!embedding) {
    43→        throw new Error("failed to create embedding for create_observation");
    44→      }
    45→
    46→      const observationRecord = await createObservation({
    47→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    48→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    49→        text: input.text,
    50→        severity: input.severity,
    51→        ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
    52→        sourceAgent: context.actor,
    53→        now: new Date(),
    54→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    55→        ...(relatedRecord ? { relatedRecord } : {}),
    56→        embedding,
    57→      });
    58→
    59→      return {
    60→        observation_id: `observation:${observationRecord.id as string}`,
    61→        severity: input.severity,
    62→        status: "open",
    63→      };
    64→    },
    65→  });
    66→}
    67→
```
