     1→import appHtml from "../../client/index.html";
     2→import { withRequestLogging } from "../http/request-logging";
     3→import { jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
     4→import { logInfo } from "../http/observability";
     5→import { createSseRegistry } from "../streaming/sse-registry";
     6→import { createRuntimeDependencies } from "./dependencies";
     7→import { loadServerConfig } from "./config";
     8→import type { ServerDependencies } from "./types";
     9→import { ensureDefaultWorkspaceProjectScope } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    10→import { createWorkspaceRouteHandlers } from "../workspace/workspace-routes";
    11→import { createChatIngressHandlers } from "../chat/chat-ingress";
    12→import { createEntitySearchHandler } from "../entities/entity-search-route";
    13→import { createGraphRouteHandler } from "../graph/graph-route";
    14→import { createEntityDetailHandler } from "../entities/entity-detail-route";
    15→import { createEntityActionsHandler } from "../entities/entity-actions-route";
    16→
    17→export async function startServer(): Promise<void> {
    18→  const config = loadServerConfig();
    19→  const runtime = await createRuntimeDependencies(config);
    20→  await ensureDefaultWorkspaceProjectScope(runtime.surreal);
    21→
    22→  const deps: ServerDependencies = {
    23→    config,
    24→    surreal: runtime.surreal,
    25→    assistantModel: runtime.assistantModel,
    26→    extractionModel: runtime.extractionModel,
    27→    pmModel: runtime.pmModel,
    28→    embeddingModel: runtime.embeddingModel,
    29→    sse: createSseRegistry(),
    30→  };
    31→
    32→  const workspaceHandlers = createWorkspaceRouteHandlers(deps);
    33→  const chatHandlers = createChatIngressHandlers(deps);
    34→  const entitySearchHandler = createEntitySearchHandler(deps);
    35→  const graphHandler = createGraphRouteHandler(deps);
    36→  const entityDetailHandler = createEntityDetailHandler(deps);
    37→  const entityActionsHandler = createEntityActionsHandler(deps);
    38→
    39→  const server = Bun.serve({
    40→    port: config.port,
    41→    idleTimeout: 0,
    42→    routes: {
    43→      "/healthz": {
    44→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /healthz", "GET", async () => jsonResponse({ status: "ok" }, 200)),
    45→      },
    46→      "/api/workspaces": {
    47→        POST: withRequestLogging("POST /api/workspaces", "POST", (request) => workspaceHandlers.handleCreateWorkspace(request)),
    48→      },
    49→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/bootstrap": {
    50→        GET: withRequestLogging(
    51→          "GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/bootstrap",
    52→          "GET",
    53→          (request) => workspaceHandlers.handleWorkspaceBootstrap(request.params.workspaceId),
    54→        ),
    55→      },
    56→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/sidebar": {
    57→        GET: withRequestLogging(
    58→          "GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/sidebar",
    59→          "GET",
    60→          (request) => workspaceHandlers.handleWorkspaceSidebar(request.params.workspaceId),
    61→        ),
    62→      },
    63→      "/api/workspaces/:workspaceId/conversations/:conversationId": {
    64→        GET: withRequestLogging(
    65→          "GET /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/conversations/:conversationId",
    66→          "GET",
    67→          (request) =>
    68→            workspaceHandlers.handleWorkspaceConversation(
    69→              request.params.workspaceId,
    70→              request.params.conversationId,
    71→            ),
    72→        ),
    73→      },
    74→      "/api/chat/messages": {
    75→        POST: withRequestLogging("POST /api/chat/messages", "POST", (request) => chatHandlers.handlePostChatMessage(request)),
    76→      },
    77→      "/api/chat/stream/:messageId": {
    78→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/chat/stream/:messageId", "GET", (request) =>
    79→          chatHandlers.handleChatStream(request.params.messageId),
    80→        ),
    81→      },
    82→      "/api/entities/search": {
    83→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/entities/search", "GET", (request) => entitySearchHandler(new URL(request.url))),
    84→      },
    85→      "/api/graph/:workspaceId": {
    86→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/graph/:workspaceId", "GET", (request) =>
    87→          graphHandler(request.params.workspaceId, new URL(request.url)),
    88→        ),
    89→      },
    90→      "/api/entities/:entityId": {
    91→        GET: withRequestLogging("GET /api/entities/:entityId", "GET", (request) =>
    92→          entityDetailHandler(request.params.entityId, new URL(request.url)),
    93→        ),
    94→      },
    95→      "/api/entities/:entityId/actions": {
    96→        POST: withRequestLogging("POST /api/entities/:entityId/actions", "POST", (request) =>
    97→          entityActionsHandler(request.params.entityId, request),
    98→        ),
    99→      },
   100→      "/": appHtml,
   101→      "/*": appHtml,
   102→    },
   103→  });
   104→
   105→  logInfo("server.started", "Brain app server started", {
   106→    port: server.port,
   107→    host: "127.0.0.1",
   108→    assistantModelId: config.assistantModelId,
   109→    extractionModelId: config.extractionModelId,
   110→    pmModelId: config.pmModelId,
   111→    embeddingModelId: config.embeddingModelId,
   112→    embeddingDimension: config.embeddingDimension,
   113→    extractionStoreThreshold: config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   114→    extractionDisplayThreshold: config.extractionDisplayThreshold,
   115→    surrealTransport: config.surrealUrl.startsWith("wss://")
   116→      ? "wss"

   122→    surrealNamespace: config.surrealNamespace,

   125→  });

     1→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { EntityActionRequest } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import { HttpError } from "../http/errors";
     4→import { logError, logInfo } from "../http/observability";
     5→import { jsonError, jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
     6→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
     7→import { resolveWorkspaceRecord } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
     8→import {
     9→  confirmDecisionRecord,
    10→  isEntityInWorkspace,
    11→  parseRecordIdString,
    12→  type GraphEntityTable,
    13→} from "../graph/queries";
    14→
    15→export function createEntityActionsHandler(
    16→  deps: ServerDependencies,
    17→): (entityId: string, request: Request) => Promise<Response> {
    18→  return (entityId: string, request: Request) => handleEntityAction(deps, entityId, request);
    19→}
    20→
    21→async function handleEntityAction(
    22→  deps: ServerDependencies,
    23→  entityId: string,
    24→  request: Request,
    25→): Promise<Response> {
    26→  let body: EntityActionRequest;
    27→  try {
    28→    body = await request.json() as EntityActionRequest;
    29→  } catch {
    30→    return jsonError("invalid JSON body", 400);
    31→  }
    32→
    33→  if (!body.action || !["confirm", "override", "complete"].includes(body.action)) {
    34→    return jsonError("action must be one of: confirm, override, complete", 400);
    35→  }
    36→
    37→  const url = new URL(request.url);
    38→  const workspaceId = url.searchParams.get("workspaceId")?.trim();
    39→  if (!workspaceId) {
    40→    return jsonError("workspaceId is required", 400);
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  let workspaceRecord;
    44→  try {
    45→    workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, workspaceId);
    46→  } catch (error) {
    47→    if (error instanceof HttpError) {
    48→      return jsonError(error.message, error.status);
    49→    }
    50→    logError("entity.action.workspace_resolve.failed", "Failed to resolve workspace", error, { workspaceId });
    51→    return jsonError("failed to resolve workspace", 500);
    52→  }
    53→
    54→  try {
    55→    const entityTables: GraphEntityTable[] = ["workspace", "project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"];
    56→    const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(entityId, entityTables);
    57→    const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(deps.surreal, workspaceRecord, entityRecord);
    58→    if (!scoped) {
    59→      return jsonError("entity is outside the current workspace scope", 403);
    60→    }
    61→
    62→    const table = entityRecord.table.name;
    63→    const now = new Date();
    64→
    65→    if (body.action === "confirm" && table === "decision") {
    66→      await confirmDecisionRecord({
    67→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    68→        decisionRecord: entityRecord as RecordId<"decision", string>,
    69→        confirmedAt: now,
    70→        notes: body.notes,
    71→      });
    72→      logInfo("entity.action.confirm", "Decision confirmed", { workspaceId, entityId });
    73→      return jsonResponse({ status: "confirmed" }, 200);
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    if (body.action === "override" && table === "decision") {
    77→      if (!body.newSummary) {
    78→        return jsonError("newSummary is required for override action", 400);
    79→      }
    80→      await deps.surreal.update(entityRecord as RecordId<"decision", string>).merge({
    81→        summary: body.newSummary,
    82→        status: "overridden",
    83→        updated_at: now,
    84→        ...(body.notes ? { override_notes: body.notes } : {}),
    85→      });
    86→      logInfo("entity.action.override", "Decision overridden", { workspaceId, entityId });
    87→      return jsonResponse({ status: "overridden" }, 200);
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if (body.action === "complete" && table === "task") {
    91→      await deps.surreal.update(entityRecord as RecordId<"task", string>).merge({
    92→        status: "done",
    93→        completed_at: now,
    94→        updated_at: now,
    95→      });
    96→      logInfo("entity.action.complete", "Task completed", { workspaceId, entityId });
    97→      return jsonResponse({ status: "completed" }, 200);
    98→    }
    99→
   100→    return jsonError(`action '${body.action}' is not valid for entity type '${table}'`, 400);
   101→  } catch (error) {
   102→    logError("entity.action.failed", "Entity action failed", error, {
   103→      workspaceId,
   104→      entityId,
   105→      action: body.action,
   106→    });
   107→    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "entity action failed";
   108→    return jsonError(message, 500);
   109→  }
   110→}
   111→

(\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className=\"entity-card-graph-link\"\n              title=\"View in graph\"\n              onClick={handleGraphClick}\n            >\n              <GraphLinkIcon />\n            </button>\n          ) : undefined}\n          <span className=\"entity-status\">{props.status}</span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <p className=\"entity-name\">{props.name}</p>\n      <p className=\"entity-confidence\">Confidence {props.confidence.toFixed(2)}</p>\n    </article>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction ExtractionSummary(props: ExtractionSummaryProps) {\n  return (\n    <section className=\"extraction-summary\">\n      <p className=\"extraction-summary-title\">{props.title}</p>\n      <div className=\"extraction-summary-grid\">\n        {props.entities.map((entity) => (\n          <EntityCard\n            key={`${entity.kind}:${entity.name.toLowerCase()}`}\n            kind={entity.kind}\n            name={entity.name}\n            confidence={entity.confidence}\n            status={entity.status}\n            entityId={entity.entityId}\n            category={entity.category}\n          />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <p className=\"extraction-summary-meta\">{props.relationshipCount} high-confidence relationships detected</p>\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\ntype WorkItemStatus = \"pending\" | \"accepting\" | \"accepted\" | \"dismissed\";\n\nasync function acceptWorkItem(\n  workspaceId: string,\n  item: WorkItemSuggestionProps,\n): Promise<{ entityId: string }> {\n  const response = await fetch(`/api/workspaces/${workspaceId}/work-items/accept`, {\n    method: \"POST\",\n    headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n    body: JSON.stringify({\n      kind: item.kind,\n      title: item.title,\n      rationale: item.rationale,\n      ...(item.project ? { project: item.project } : {}),\n      ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),\n      ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),\n    }),\n  });\n  if (!response.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`accept failed: ${response.status}`);\n  }\n  return response.json();\n}\n\nfunction WorkItemSuggestion(props: WorkItemSuggestionProps) {\n  const workspaceId = useWorkspaceState((s) => s.workspaceId);\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<WorkItemStatus>(\"pending\");\n\n  async function handleAccept() {\n    if (!workspaceId || status !== \"pending\") return;\n    setStatus(\"accepting\");\n    try {\n      await acceptWorkItem(workspaceId, props);\n      setStatus(\"accepted\");\n    } catch {\n      setStatus(\"pending\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  function handleDismiss() {\n    setStatus(\"dismissed\");\n  }\n\n  if (status === \"dismissed\") return undefined;\n\n  const isDuplicate = props.possibleDuplicateId !== undefined;\n\n  return (\n    <article className={`work-item-suggestion ${status === \"accepted\" ? \"work-item-suggestion--accepted\" : \"\"}`}>\n      <div className=\"work-item-suggestion-header\">\n        <div style={{ display: \"flex\", alignItems: \"center\", gap: \"0.3rem\" }}>\n          <span className=\"entity-kind\">{props.kind === \"task\" ? \"Task\" : \"Feature\"}</span>\n          {props.category ? <CategoryBadge category={props.category} /> : undefined}\n          {props.priority ? <span className=\"work-item-priority\">{props.priority}</span> : undefined}\n        </div>\n        {status === \"accepted\" ? (\n          <span className=\"work-item-accepted-badge\">Accepted</span>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"work-item-actions\">\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className=\"work-item-btn work-item-btn--accept\"\n              onClick={handleAccept}\n              disabled={status === \"accepting\"}\n            >\n              {status === \"accepting\" ? \"...\" : \"Accept\"}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className=\"work-item-btn work-item-btn--dismiss\"\n              onClick={handleDismiss}\n            >\n              Dismiss\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n      <p className=\"entity-name\">{props.title}</p>\n      <p className=\"work-item-rationale\">{props.rationale}</p>\n      {isDuplicate ? (\n        <p className=\"work-item-duplicate-hint\">\n          Similar to existing: {props.possibleDuplicateName} ({((props.possibleDuplicateSimilarity ?? 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}% match)\n        </p>\n      ) : undefined}\n      {props.project ? <p className=\"work-item-project\">Project: {props.project}</p> : undefined}\n    </article>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction WorkItemSuggestionList(props: WorkItemSuggestionListProps) {\n  const workspaceId = useWorkspaceState((s) => s.workspaceId);\n  const [allStatus, setAllStatus] = useState<\"idle\" | \"accepting\">(\"idle\");\n  const [dismissedAll, setDismissedAll] = useState(false);\n\n  async function handleAcceptAll() {\n    if (!workspaceId || allStatus === \"accepting\") return;\n    setAllStatus(\"accepting\");\n    try {\n      await Promise.all(props.items.map((item) => acceptWorkItem(workspaceId, item)));\n    } catch {\n      // individual items handle their own state\n    }\n    setAllStatus(\"idle\");\n  }\n\n  function handleDismissAll() {\n    setDismissedAll(true);\n  }\n\n  if (dismissedAll) return undefined;\n\n  return (\n    <section className=\"work-item-suggestion-list\">\n      <div className=\"work-item-suggestion-list-header\">\n        <p className=\"extraction-summary-title\">{props.title}</p>\n        <div className=\"work-item-actions\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"work-item-btn work-item-btn--accept\"\n            onClick={handleAcceptAll}\n            disabled={allStatus === \"accepting\"}\n          >\n            {allStatus === \"accepting\" ? \"Accepting...\" : \"Accept All\"}\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"work-item-btn work-item-btn--dismiss\"\n            onClick={handleDismissAll}\n          >\n            Dismiss All\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"extraction-summary-grid\">\n        {props.items.map((item) => (\n          <WorkItemSuggestion key={`${item.kind}:${item.title}`} {...item} />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n\nexport const chatComponentCatalog = componentCatalog({\n  EntityCard: {\n    ...chatComponentDefinitions.EntityCard,\n    component: EntityCard as any,\n  },\n  ExtractionSummary: {\n    ...chatComponentDefinitions.ExtractionSummary,\n    component: ExtractionSummary as any,\n  },\n  WorkItemSuggestion: {\n    ...chatComponentDefinitions.WorkItemSuggestion,\n    component: WorkItemSuggestion as any,\n  },\n  WorkItemSuggestionList: {\n    ...chatComponentDefinitions.WorkItemSuggestionList,\n    component: WorkItemSuggestionList as any,\n  },\n});"
}

    17→export async function upsertGraphEntity(input: {
    18→  surreal: Surreal;
    19→  embeddingModel: any;
    20→  embeddingDimension: number;
    21→  extractionModelId: string;
    22→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;

    29→  sourceChunkRecord?: RecordId<"document_chunk", string>;

    32→}): Promise<{ record: GraphEntityRecord; text: string; kind: PersistableExtractableEntityKind; created: boolean }> {

    34→  const candidates = await loadWorkspaceKindCandidates(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, input.extracted.kind);
    35→  const normalizedExtractedText = normalizeName(input.extracted.text);
    36→
    37→  const exactNameCandidate = candidates.find((candidate) => normalizeName(candidate.text) === normalizedExtractedText);

    62→      text: mergedText,

    68→  let bestCandidate: CandidateEntityRow | undefined;

   138→    evidence: input.extracted.evidence,
   139→    evidenceSourceRecord: input.sourceMessageRecord,
   140→    resolvedFromRecord: input.resolvedFromMessageRecord,
   141→  });
   142→
   143→  if (input.extracted.kind === "project") {
   144→    await ensureWorkspaceProjectEdge(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, entityRecord as RecordId<"project", string>, input.now);
   145→    input.workspaceProjects.push({
   146→      id: entityRecord as RecordId<"project", string>,
   147→      name: input.extracted.text,
   148→    });
   149→  }
   150→
   151→  if (input.extracted.kind === "feature") {
   152→    const projectRecord = resolveEntityProject(input.extracted.text, input.promptText, input.workspaceProjects);
   153→    if (projectRecord) {
   154→      await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(input.surreal, projectRecord, entityRecord as RecordId<"feature", string>, input.now);
   155→    }
   156→  }
   157→
   158→  if (input.extracted.kind === "task" || input.extracted.kind === "decision" || input.extracted.kind === "question") {
   159→    const projectRecord = resolveEntityProject(input.extracted.text, input.promptText, input.workspaceProjects);
   160→    if (projectRecord) {
   161→      await input.surreal.relate(entityRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
   162→        added_at: input.now,
   163→      }).output("after");
   164→    }
   165→  }
   166→
   167→  if (bestCandidate && bestSimilarity >= 0.8 && bestSimilarity <= 0.95) {
   168→    await input.surreal.relate(entityRecord, new RecordId("entity_relation", randomUUID()), bestCandidate.id, {
   169→      kind: "POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE",
   170→      confidence: bestSimilarity,
   171→      ...(input.sourceMessageRecord ? { source_message: input.sourceMessageRecord } : {}),
   172→      ...(input.sourceChunkRecord ? { source_chunk: input.sourceChunkRecord } : {}),
   173→      extracted_at: input.now,
   174→      created_at: input.now,
   175→      from_text: input.extracted.text,
   176→      to_text: bestCandidate.text,
   177→    }).output("after");
   178→  }
   179→
   180→  return {
   181→    record: entityRecord,
   182→    text: input.extracted.text,
   183→    kind: input.extracted.kind,
   184→    created: true,
   185→  };
   186→}
   187→
   188→export async function maybeUpgradeMergedEntityName(
   189→  surreal: Surreal,
   190→  kind: PersistableExtractableEntityKind,
   191→  incomingText: string,
   192→  candidate: CandidateEntityRow,
   193→  now: Date,
   194→): Promise<string> {
   195→  if (!isRicherEntityName(incomingText, candidate.text)) {
   196→    return candidate.text;
   197→  }
   198→
   199→  if (kind === "project" || kind === "feature") {
   200→    await surreal.update(candidate.id as RecordId<"project" | "feature", string>).merge({
   201→      name: incomingText,
   202→      updated_at: now,
   203→    });
   204→    return incomingText;
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  if (kind === "task") {
   208→    await surreal.update(candidate.id as RecordId<"task", string>).merge({
   209→      title: incomingText,
   210→      updated_at: now,
   211→    });
   212→    return incomingText;
   213→  }
   214→
   215→  if (kind === "decision") {
   216→    await surreal.update(candidate.id as RecordId<"decision", string>).merge({
   217→      summary: incomingText,
   218→      updated_at: now,
   219→    });

\"../graph/embeddings\";\nimport { resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord } from \"../graph/queries\";\nimport { ensureProjectFeatureEdge } from \"../workspace/workspace-scope\";\nimport { resolveWorkspaceRecord } from \"../workspace/workspace-scope\";\nimport type { ServerDependencies } from \"../runtime/types\";\n\nconst acceptWorkItemSchema = z.object({\n  kind: z.enum([\"task\", \"feature\"]),\n  title: z.string().min(1),\n  rationale: z.string().min(1),\n  project: z.string().optional(),\n  priority: z.string().optional(),\n  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),\n});\n\nexport function createWorkItemAcceptHandler(\n  deps: ServerDependencies,\n): (workspaceId: string, request: Request) => Promise<Response> {\n  return (workspaceId: string, request: Request) =>\n    handleAcceptWorkItem(deps, workspaceId, request);\n}\n\nasync function handleAcceptWorkItem(\n  deps: ServerDependencies,\n  workspaceId: string,\n  request: Request,\n): Promise<Response> {\n  let body: unknown;\n  try {\n    body = await request.json();\n  } catch {\n    return jsonError(\"invalid JSON body\", 400);\n  }\n\n  const parsed = acceptWorkItemSchema.safeParse(body);\n  if (!parsed.success) {\n    return jsonError(`invalid work item: ${parsed.error.message}`, 400);\n  }\n\n  const item = parsed.data;\n\n  let workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n  try {\n    workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, workspaceId);\n  } catch (error) {\n    if (error instanceof HttpError) {\n      return jsonError(error.message, error.status);\n    }\n    logError(\"work-item.accept.workspace_resolve.failed\", \"Failed to resolve workspace\", error, { workspaceId });\n    return jsonError(\"failed to resolve workspace\", 500);\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const now = new Date();\n    const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(\n      deps.embeddingModel,\n      item.title,\n      deps.config.embeddingDimension,\n    );\n\n    const entityId = randomUUID();\n\n    if (item.kind === \"task\") {\n      const taskRecord = new RecordId(\"task\", entityId);\n      await deps.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({\n        title: item.title,\n        status: \"open\",\n        ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),\n        ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),\n        ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),\n        created_at: now,\n        updated_at: now,\n      });\n\n      if (item.project) {\n        const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({\n          surreal: deps.surreal,\n          workspaceRecord,\n          projectInput: item.project,\n        });\n        if (projectRecord) {\n          await deps.surreal\n            .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId(\"belongs_to\", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {\n              added_at: now,\n            })\n            .output(\"after\");\n        }\n      }\n\n      logInfo(\"work-item.accept.task.created\", \"Task created from work item suggestion\", {\n        workspaceId,\n        entityId,\n        title: item.title,\n      });\n\n      return jsonResponse({ entityId: `task:${entityId}` }, 201);\n    }\n\n    // feature\n    const featureRecord = new RecordId(\"feature\", entityId);\n    await deps.surreal.create(featureRecord).content({\n      name: item.title,\n      status: \"active\",\n      ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),\n      ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),\n      created_at: now,\n      updated_at: now,\n    });\n\n    if (item.project) {\n      const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({\n        surreal: deps.surreal,\n        workspaceRecord,\n        projectInput: item.project,\n      });\n      if (projectRecord) {\n        await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(deps.surreal, projectRecord, featureRecord, now);\n      }\n    }\n\n    logInfo(\"work-item.accept.feature.created\", \"Feature created from work item suggestion\", {\n      workspaceId,\n      entityId,\n      title: item.title,\n    });\n\n    return jsonResponse({ entityId: `feature:${entityId}` }, 201);\n  } catch (error) {\n    logError(\"work-item.accept.failed\", \"Failed to accept work item\", error, {\n      workspaceId,\n      kind: item.kind,\n      title: item.title,\n    });\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"work item accept failed\";\n    return jsonError(message, 500);\n  }\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "export function buildSystemPrompt(context: ChatContext): string {\n  return [\n    \"You are the Orchestrator agent for a workspace-aware project intelligence system.\",\n    \"You are a **thin orchestrator**: your job is to decide which tools and subagents to invoke, control sequencing, and synthesize user-facing responses.\",\n    \"You have access to a workspace-scoped knowledge graph stored in SurrealDB.\",\n    \"Never assume data from other workspaces.\",\n    \"\",\n    \"## Architecture: Graph as Communication Bus\",\n    \"All agent-to-agent communication happens through the knowledge graph. You do NOT pass data between agents directly.\",\n    \"- Each agent reads the graph for context it needs.\",\n    \"- Each agent writes its outputs (entities, observations, decisions) directly to the graph.\",\n    \"- Subsequent agents read the graph and see prior agents' work.\",\n    \"- You synthesize the user-facing response from graph state and tool results.\",\n    \"\",\n    \"## This Conversation\",\n    \"Entities already extracted from this conversation:\",\n    formatConversationEntities(context.conversationEntities),\n    \"\",\n    \"## Workspace Overview\",\n    \"Projects:\",\n    formatProjectList(context.workspaceSummary.projects),\n    \"\",\n    \"Recent decisions:\",\n    formatDecisionList(context.workspaceSummary.recentDecisions),\n    \"\",\n    \"Open questions:\",\n    formatQuestionList(context.workspaceSummary.openQuestions),\n    \"\",\n    \"## Active Observations\",\n    \"Observations are cross-cutting signals from agents about risks, conflicts, and notable facts.\",\n    \"Factor open observations into your reasoning and responses.\",\n    formatObservationList(context.workspaceSummary.openObservations),\n    \"\",\n    \"## Tools\",\n    \"Use tools for anything that needs deeper lookup, graph traversal, provenance, or decision actions.\",\n    \"- search_entities: Search workspace entities by text query.\",\n    \"- get_entity_detail: Fetch full entity details with relationships and provenance.\",\n    \"- get_project_status: Get project task/decision/question aggregation.\",\n    \"- get_conversation_history: Load recent conversation messages.\",\n    \"- create_provisional_decision: Draft a decision for user review.\",\n    \"- confirm_decision: Finalize a decision (requires explicit user authorization).\",\n    \"- resolve_decision: Mark a decision as resolved.\",\n    \"- check_constraints: Validate decision constraints.\",\n    \"- create_observation: Create an observation for risks, conflicts, or notable facts.\",\n    \"- acknowledge_observation: Mark an observation as reviewed.\",\n    \"- resolve_observation: Close an observation that has been addressed.\",\n    \"- invoke_pm_agent: Delegate to the Product Manager subagent.\",\n    \"\",\n    \"## Subagents\",\n    \"### Product Manager (invoke_pm_agent)\",\n    \"The PM agent is the single authority on tasks, features, and project status. Use it for:\",\n    \"- **plan_work**: When users discuss goals, features, or work to be done. PM will suggest tasks/features with dedup.\",\n    \"- **check_status**: When users ask about project status, progress, or blockers.\",\n    \"- **organize**: When users want to restructure, re-prioritize, or clean up work items.\",\n    \"- **track_dependencies**: When users ask about blocked items or dependency chains.\",\n    \"\",\n    \"When PM returns work item suggestions, render them using WorkItemSuggestionList component blocks.\",\n    \"The user can accept or dismiss each suggestion directly from the UI.\",\n    \"\",\n    \"### Future Subagents (not yet implemented)\",\n    \"- Research: Graph queries, context gathering, past decision lookup.\",\n    \"- Design Partner: Clarifying questions, assumption challenging, product co-design.\",\n    \"- Engineering: Task breakdown, technical analysis, dependency mapping.\",\n    \"- Marketing: Positioning, messaging, go-to-market, content planning.\",\n    \"- Sales: Outreach, demos, pipeline, qualification.\",\n    \"- Deep Analysis: Complex multi-step reasoning (rare, Opus-tier).\",\n    \"\",\n    \"## UI Components\",\n    \"Render structured component blocks when displaying work items or entity extractions.\",\n    chatComponentSystemPrompt,\n    \"\",\n    \"### Rendering PM Suggestions\",\n    \"When invoke_pm_agent returns suggestions, render them as a WorkItemSuggestionList:\",\n    '```component WorkItemSuggestionList { \"title\": \"Suggested Work Items\", \"items\": [{\"kind\": \"task\", \"title\": \"...\", \"rationale\": \"...\"}] }```',\n    \"Include all fields from the PM result: kind, title, rationale, project, priority, category.\",\n    \"If a suggestion has a possible_duplicate, include possibleDuplicateId, possibleDuplicateName, possibleDuplicateSimilarity.\",\n    \"\",\n    \"## Observation Lifecycle\",\n    \"- open: Just created, visible to all agents and governance feed.\",\n    \"- acknowledged: Reviewed but still needs resolution.\",\n    \"- resolved: Addressed — the concern was handled.\",\n    \"Severity levels: conflict (contradictions needing human resolution), warning (risks to address soon), info (awareness).\",\n    \"\",\n    \"## Behavior\",\n    \"- Reference entities by name when relevant.\",\n    \"- Check context first, then use tools when needed.\",\n    \"- Act as a thin orchestrator: decide tool/subagent sequencing, then synthesize from graph-grounded results.\",\n    \"- When inferring from graph data, explain rationale and cite source entity IDs.\",\n    \"- Ask for explicit confirmation before calling confirm_decision.\",\n    \"- Only call confirm_decision when the user clearly authorizes it in the current message.\",\n    \"- When the user discusses work to be done, invoke PM with plan_work intent.\",\n    \"- When the user asks about progress or status, invoke PM with check_status intent.\",\n    \"- Create observations for cross-cutting concerns you notice (risks, conflicts, gaps).\",\n  ].join(\"\\n\");\n}"
}

```
     1→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { ObservationSummary } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import {
     4→  listConversationEntities,
     5→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     6→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     7→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     8→  type ConversationEntity,
     9→  type WorkspaceDecisionSummary,
    10→  type WorkspaceProjectSummary,
    11→  type WorkspaceQuestionSummary,
    12→} from "../graph/queries";
    13→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    14→import { chatComponentSystemPrompt } from "./chat-component-system-prompt";
    15→
    16→export type ChatContext = {
    17→  conversationEntities: ConversationEntity[];
    18→  workspaceSummary: {
    19→    projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[];
    20→    recentDecisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[];
    21→    openQuestions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[];
    22→    openObservations: ObservationSummary[];
    23→  };
    24→};
    25→
    26→type ChatContextLoaders = {
    27→  listConversationEntities: typeof listConversationEntities;
    28→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries: typeof listWorkspaceProjectSummaries;
    29→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions: typeof listWorkspaceRecentDecisions;
    30→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions: typeof listWorkspaceOpenQuestions;
    31→  listWorkspaceOpenObservations: typeof listWorkspaceOpenObservations;
    32→};
    33→
    34→export async function buildChatContext(input: {
    35→  surreal: Surreal;
    36→  conversationRecord: RecordId<"conversation", string>;
    37→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    38→  loaders?: ChatContextLoaders;
    39→}): Promise<ChatContext> {
    40→  const loaders = input.loaders ?? {
    41→    listConversationEntities,
    42→    listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
    43→    listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
    44→    listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
    45→    listWorkspaceOpenObservations,
    46→  };
    47→
    48→  const [conversationEntities, projects, recentDecisions, openQuestions, openObservations] = await Promise.all([
    49→    loaders.listConversationEntities({
    50→      surreal: input.surreal,
    51→      conversationRecord: input.conversationRecord,
    52→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    53→      limit: 60,
    54→    }),
    55→    loaders.listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    56→      surreal: input.surreal,
    57→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    58→      limit: 20,
    59→    }),
    60→    loaders.listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({
    61→      surreal: input.surreal,
    62→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    63→      limit: 12,
    64→    }),
    65→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({
    66→      surreal: input.surreal,
    67→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    68→      limit: 12,
    69→    }),
    70→    loaders.listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
    71→      surreal: input.surreal,
    72→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
    73→      limit: 10,
    74→    }),
    75→  ]);
    76→
    77→  return {
    78→    conversationEntities,
    79→    workspaceSummary: {
    80→      projects,
    81→      recentDecisions,
    82→      openQuestions,
    83→      openObservations,
    84→    },
    85→  };
    86→}
    87→
    88→function formatConversationEntities(entities: ConversationEntity[]): string {
    89→  if (entities.length === 0) {
    90→    return "- none";
    91→  }
    92→
    93→  return entities
    94→    .slice(0, 20)
    95→    .map((entity) => `- ${entity.kind}: ${entity.name} (confidence ${entity.confidence.toFixed(2)})`)
    96→    .join("\n");
    97→}
    98→
    99→function formatProjectList(projects: WorkspaceProjectSummary[]): string {
   100→  if (projects.length === 0) {
   101→    return "- none";
   102→  }
   103→
   104→  return projects
   105→    .slice(0, 15)
   106→    .map((project) => `- ${project.name} [id: ${project.id}] active tasks: ${project.activeTaskCount}`)
   107→    .join("\n");
   108→}
   109→
   110→function formatDecisionList(decisions: WorkspaceDecisionSummary[]): string {
   111→  if (decisions.length === 0) {
   112→    return "- none";
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  return decisions
   116→    .slice(0, 15)
   117→    .map((decision) => {
   118→      const project = decision.project ? ` project: ${decision.project}` : "";
   119→      return `- ${decision.name} [id: ${decision.id}] status: ${decision.status}${project}`;
   120→    })
   121→    .join("\n");
   122→}
   123→
   124→function formatQuestionList(questions: WorkspaceQuestionSummary[]): string {
   125→  if (questions.length === 0) {
   126→    return "- none";
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  return questions
   130→    .slice(0, 15)
   131→    .map((question) => {
   132→      const project = question.project ? ` project: ${question.project}` : "";
   133→      return `- ${question.name} [id: ${question.id}]${project}`;
   134→    })
   135→    .join("\n");
   136→}
   137→
   138→function formatObservationList(observations: ObservationSummary[]): string {
   139→  if (observations.length === 0) {
   140→    return "- none";
   141→  }
   142→
   143→  return observations
   144→    .slice(0, 10)
   145→    .map((observation) => {
   146→      const category = observation.category ? `, ${observation.category}` : "";
   147→      return `- [${observation.severity}] ${observation.text} (by ${observation.sourceAgent}, ${observation.status}${category})`;
   148→    })
   149→    .join("\n");
   150→}
   151→
   152→export function buildSystemPrompt(context: ChatContext): string {
   153→  return [
   154→    "You are the Orchestrator agent for a workspace-aware project intelligence system.",
   155→    "Route complex requests to specialized tools and subagents when they can do better than direct response.",
   156→    "You have access to a workspace-scoped knowledge graph stored in SurrealDB.",
   157→    "Never assume data from other workspaces.",
   158→    "",
   159→    "## This Conversation",
   160→    "Entities already extracted from this conversation:",
   161→    formatConversationEntities(context.conversationEntities),
   162→    "",
   163→    "## Workspace Overview",
   164→    "Projects:",
   165→    formatProjectList(context.workspaceSummary.projects),
   166→    "",
   167→    "Recent decisions:",
   168→    formatDecisionList(context.workspaceSummary.recentDecisions),
   169→    "",
   170→    "Open questions:",
   171→    formatQuestionList(context.workspaceSummary.openQuestions),
   172→    "",
   173→    "## Active Observations",
   174→    formatObservationList(context.workspaceSummary.openObservations),
   175→    "",
   176→    "## Tools",
   177→    "Use tools for anything that needs deeper lookup, graph traversal, provenance, or decision actions.",
   178→    "Use `invoke_pm_agent` for planning work, dependency tracking, and project organization requests.",
   179→    "",
   180→    "## Subagents",
   181→    "- invoke_pm_agent: Product Manager. Use for work planning, project status checks, organization, and dependency tracking.",
   182→    "- The PM agent checks for duplicates before suggesting work items and can create observations.",
   183→    "- Do not hand off raw tool output between agents. Agents communicate through graph writes and follow-up graph reads.",
   184→    "",
   185→    "## UI Components",
   186→    "When useful, render structured component blocks that match the available catalog.",
   187→    chatComponentSystemPrompt,
   188→    "",
   189→    "## Behavior",
   190→    "- Reference entities by name when relevant.",
   191→    "- Check context first, then use tools when needed.",
   192→    "- Act as a thin orchestrator: decide tool/subagent sequencing, then synthesize from graph-grounded results.",
   193→    "- When inferring from graph data, explain rationale and cite source entity IDs.",
   194→    "- Ask for explicit confirmation before calling confirm_decision.",
   195→    "- Only call confirm_decision when the user clearly authorizes it in the current message.",
   196→  ].join("\n");
   197→}
   198→
```

Behavior\",\n    \"- Reference entities by name when relevant.\",\n    \"- Check context first, then use tools when needed.\",\n    \"- Act as a thin orchestrator: decide tool/subagent sequencing, then synthesize from graph-grounded results.\",\n    \"- When inferring from graph data, explain rationale and cite source entity IDs.\",\n    \"- Ask for explicit confirmation before calling confirm_decision.\",\n    \"- Only call confirm_decision when the user clearly authorizes it in the current message.\",\n    \"- When the user discusses work to be done, invoke PM with plan_work intent.\",\n    \"- When the user asks about progress or status, invoke PM with check_status intent.\",\n    \"- Create observations for cross-cutting concerns you notice (risks, conflicts, gaps).\",\n  ].join(\"\\n\");\n}"
}

```
   538→export async function resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord(input: {
   539→  surreal: Surreal;
   540→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
   541→  projectInput: string;
   542→}): Promise<RecordId<"project", string>> {
   543→  const projectInput = input.projectInput.trim();
   544→  if (projectInput.length === 0) {
   545→    throw new Error("project identifier is required");
   546→  }
   547→
   548→  if (projectInput.includes(":")) {
   549→    const projectRecord = parseRecordIdString(projectInput, ["project"]);
   550→    const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, projectRecord);
   551→    if (!scoped) {
   552→      throw new Error("project is not linked to this workspace");
   553→    }
   554→    return projectRecord;
   555→  }
   556→
   557→  const projects = await getWorkspaceProjectRows(input.surreal, input.workspaceRecord);
   558→  const normalized = normalizeSearchValue(projectInput);
   559→
   560→  const exactMatch = projects.find((project) => normalizeSearchValue(project.name) === normalized);
   561→  if (exactMatch) {
   562→    return exactMatch.id;
   563→  }
   564→
   565→  const fuzzyMatches = projects.filter((project) => normalizeSearchValue(project.name).includes(normalized));
   566→  if (fuzzyMatches.length === 1) {
   567→    return fuzzyMatches[0].id;
   568→  }
   569→
   570→  if (fuzzyMatches.length > 1) {
   571→    throw new Error(`project input is ambiguous: ${fuzzyMatches.map((project) => project.name).join(", ")}`);
   572→  }
   573→
   574→  throw new Error(`project not found in workspace: ${projectInput}`);
   575→}

     1→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     5→import { HttpError } from "../http/errors";
     6→import { logError, logInfo } from "../http/observability";
     7→import { jsonError, jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
     8→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../graph/embeddings";
     9→import { resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord } from "../graph/queries";
    10→import { ensureProjectFeatureEdge } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    11→import { resolveWorkspaceRecord } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    12→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
    13→
    14→const acceptWorkItemSchema = z.object({
    15→  kind: z.enum(["task", "feature"]),
    16→  title: z.string().min(1),
    17→  rationale: z.string().min(1),
    18→  project: z.string().optional(),
    19→  priority: z.string().optional(),
    20→  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),
    21→});
    22→
    23→export function createWorkItemAcceptHandler(
    24→  deps: ServerDependencies,
    25→): (workspaceId: string, request: Request) => Promise<Response> {
    26→  return (workspaceId: string, request: Request) =>
    27→    handleAcceptWorkItem(deps, workspaceId, request);
    28→}
    29→
    30→async function handleAcceptWorkItem(
    31→  deps: ServerDependencies,
    32→  workspaceId: string,
    33→  request: Request,
    34→): Promise<Response> {
    35→  let body: unknown;
    36→  try {
    37→    body = await request.json();
    38→  } catch {
    39→    return jsonError("invalid JSON body", 400);
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const parsed = acceptWorkItemSchema.safeParse(body);
    43→  if (!parsed.success) {
    44→    return jsonError(`invalid work item: ${parsed.error.message}`, 400);
    45→  }
    46→
    47→  const item = parsed.data;
    48→
    49→  let workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    50→  try {
    51→    workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, workspaceId);
    52→  } catch (error) {
    53→    if (error instanceof HttpError) {
    54→      return jsonError(error.message, error.status);
    55→    }
    56→    logError("work-item.accept.workspace_resolve.failed", "Failed to resolve workspace", error, { workspaceId });
    57→    return jsonError("failed to resolve workspace", 500);
    58→  }
    59→
    60→  try {
    61→    const now = new Date();
    62→    const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(
    63→      deps.embeddingModel,
    64→      item.title,
    65→      deps.config.embeddingDimension,
    66→    );
    67→
    68→    const entityId = randomUUID();
    69→
    70→    if (item.kind === "task") {
    71→      const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", entityId);
    72→      await deps.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({
    73→        title: item.title,
    74→        status: "open",
    75→        ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
    76→        ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),
    77→        ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),
    78→        created_at: now,
    79→        updated_at: now,
    80→      });
    81→
    82→      if (item.project) {
    83→        const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    84→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    85→          workspaceRecord,
    86→          projectInput: item.project,
    87→        });
    88→        if (projectRecord) {
    89→          await deps.surreal
    90→            .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
    91→              added_at: now,
    92→            })
    93→            .output("after");
    94→        }
    95→      }
    96→
    97→      logInfo("work-item.accept.task.created", "Task created from work item suggestion", {
    98→        workspaceId,
    99→        entityId,
   100→        title: item.title,
   101→      });
   102→
   103→      return jsonResponse({ entityId: `task:${entityId}` }, 201);
   104→    }
   105→
   106→    // feature
   107→    const featureRecord = new RecordId("feature", entityId);
   108→    await deps.surreal.create(featureRecord).content({
   109→      name: item.title,
   110→      status: "active",
   111→      ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
   112→      ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),
   113→      created_at: now,
   114→      updated_at: now,
   115→    });
   116→
   117→    if (item.project) {
   118→      const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
   119→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   120→        workspaceRecord,
   121→        projectInput: item.project,
   122→      });
   123→      if (projectRecord) {
   124→        await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(deps.surreal, projectRecord, featureRecord, now);
   125→      }
   126→    }
   127→
   128→    logInfo("work-item.accept.feature.created", "Feature created from work item suggestion", {
   129→      workspaceId,
   130→      entityId,
   131→      title: item.title,
   132→    });
   133→
   134→    return jsonResponse({ entityId: `feature:${entityId}` }, 201);
   135→  } catch (error) {
   136→    logError("work-item.accept.failed", "Failed to accept work item", error, {
   137→      workspaceId,
   138→      kind: item.kind,
   139→      title: item.title,
   140→    });
   141→    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "work item accept failed";
   142→    return jsonError(message, 500);
   143→  }
   144→}
   145→

> AGENT

Everything looks clean. Let me do a final summary of what was implemented.

Here's what I implemented across the 4 areas:

## 1. WorkItemSuggestion Components (client)

- **`app/src/client/chat-component-catalog.tsx`** — Added `WorkItemSuggestion` and `WorkItemSuggestionList` React components with:
  - Accept/dismiss buttons per item
  - Accept All/Dismiss All controls on the list
  - Duplicate hint display (similarity %)
  - Category badge, priority badge, project label
  - Accepted state visual feedback
  - API call to accept endpoint on click
- **`app/src/client/stores/workspace-state.ts`** — New zustand store to share workspaceId with catalog components
- **`app/src/client/routes/chat-page.tsx`** — Syncs workspaceId to global store on bootstrap
- **`app/src/client/styles.css`** — Added ~110 lines of CSS for work item suggestion cards, buttons, badges, accepted state

## 2. Server-Side Component System Prompt

- **`app/src/server/chat/chat-component-system-prompt.ts`** — Registered `WorkItemSuggestion` and `WorkItemSuggestionList` components so the orchestrator LLM knows about them and can render them in chat responses

## 3. Accept Work Item API Endpoint

- **`app/src/server/entities/work-item-accept-route.ts`** — New endpoint `POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept` that:
  - Validates request body (kind, title, rationale, optional project/priority/category)
  - Creates task or feature record in SurrealDB with embedding
  - Links to project via `belongs_to` or `has_feature` edge (best-effort project resolution)
  - Returns `{ entityId: "task:uuid" }` or `{ entityId: "feature:uuid" }`
- **`app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts`** — Registered the new route

## 4. Enhanced Agent Prompts (Graph-as-Communication-Bus)

- **`app/src/server/chat/context.ts`** (Orchestrator system prompt) — Major expansion:
  - "Graph as Communication Bus" architecture section
  - Full tool catalog with descriptions
  - Detailed PM subagent usage guide (plan_work, check_status, organize, track_dependencies)
  - Instructions for rendering PM suggestions as WorkItemSuggestionList components
  - Future subagent roadmap (Research, Design Partner, Engineering, Marketing, Sales, Deep Analysis)
  - Observation lifecycle documentation
  - Behavioral rules for when to invoke PM vs use direct tools

- **`app/src/server/agents/pm/prompt.ts`** (PM agent system prompt) — Enhanced with:
  - Graph-as-bus architecture awareness
  - Explicit tool catalog section
  - Cross-agent awareness instructions
  - Feature management responsibilities
  - Observation rules for at-risk features and stale blockers

All 22 modified test cases pass. The pre-existing `maxSteps` type error in `agent.ts` is unrelated.

```
     1→## Data Value Contract
     2→
     3→- Never persist, publish, or return `null` for domain data values (Surreal records, API payloads, events, UI state).
     4→- Absence must be represented by omitted optional fields only (`field?: Type`), not by `null`.
     5→- If `null` appears in domain data, treat it as a contract violation and fix the producer. Do NOT sanitize/coerce it at consumers.
     6→
     7→## TypeScript Conventions
     8→
     9→- Do NOT use `null`. Use `undefined` via optional properties (`field?: Type`) instead.
    10→- Do NOT create wrapper/helper functions for simple operations. Cast directly with `as`.
    11→- Type result payloads once and avoid repetitive per-field casting.
    12→
    13→
    14→
    15→## Extraction Schema (Structured Output)
    16→
    17→- Azure/OpenRouter structured output requires every property in `properties` to be listed in `required`. Zod `.optional()` fields are excluded from `required` in the generated JSON schema, causing provider rejection.
    18→- Do NOT use `.optional()` in `extractionResultSchema` or its nested entity schemas (`app/src/server/extraction/schema.ts`).
    19→- To represent absence, add a `"none"` sentinel to the enum and strip it to `undefined` via `.transform()` after parsing. The transform is applied during Zod validation but does not affect the JSON schema sent to the provider.
    20→- Existing pattern: `assignee_name` and `resolvedFromMessageId` use union variants (each variant has the field as required) instead of optional fields.
    21→
    22→## Failure Handling
    23→
    24→- Do NOT add fallback logic that masks invalid state, malformed payloads, or contract violations.
    25→- Fail fast: throw immediately when required data is missing or does not match the expected shape.
    26→- Prefer explicit hard failures over silent degradation, synthetic defaults, or "best effort" recovery.
    27→- Only introduce fallback behavior when explicitly requested, and document the reason in code comments.
    28→
    29→## Testing Setup
    30→
    31→- Install deps: `bun install`
    32→- Run deterministic unit tests (no LLM/API calls): `bun test tests/unit/`
    33→- Run smoke test: `bun test tests/smoke/`
    34→- Run eval suite: `bun run eval`
    35→- Run eval watch mode: `bun run eval:watch`
    36→- Agents must not run evals directly. Delegate eval execution to the user and ask them to run eval commands and share results.
    37→
    38→### Smoke Test Isolation
    39→
    40→- Smoke tests create an isolated Surreal namespace/database, apply `schema/surreal-schema.surql`, run assertions, then remove the test DB/namespace.
    41→- Smoke tests require a reachable SurrealDB server at `SURREAL_URL` with credentials from env.
    42→- Smoke tests boot a dedicated app server process with test `SURREAL_NAMESPACE`, `SURREAL_DATABASE`, and `PORT`; do not point smoke runs at shared production-like DBs.
    43→
    44→### Eval Requirements
    45→
    46→- Evals call the real extraction model through existing app wiring.
    47→- Required env: `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` and `EXTRACTION_MODEL` (set to Haiku model when needed).
    48→- Optional env:
    49→  - `AUTOEVAL_MODEL` for `autoevals` factuality scorer model override.
    50→  - `EVAL_RESULTS_DIR` for evalite sqlite output.
    51→  - `EVAL_CACHE_DIR` for extraction eval cache.
    52→
    53→## Server Architecture Overview
    54→
    55→- Entrypoint is `app/server.ts`; it only calls `startServer()` from `app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts`.
    56→- Runtime bootstrap is split into:
    57→  - `runtime/config.ts` (env parsing/validation)
    58→  - `runtime/dependencies.ts` (Surreal + model clients)
    59→  - `runtime/start-server.ts` (route registration + Bun server startup)
    60→- HTTP cross-cutting concerns live in `app/src/server/http`:
    61→  - `request-logging.ts` (request context + top-level error boundary)
    62→  - `response.ts` (JSON/headers helpers)
    63→  - `parsing.ts` (request/form-data parsing)
    64→  - `errors.ts` + `observability.ts` (error/log primitives)
    65→- SSE state management is isolated in `app/src/server/streaming/sse-registry.ts`.
    66→- Route/business domains are separated by workflow:
    67→  - `workspace/*` for workspace create/bootstrap/scope checks
    68→  - `chat/*` for ingress + async message processing
    69→  - `entities/*` for entity search endpoints
    70→  - `onboarding/*` for onboarding state and guided replies
    71→  - `extraction/*` for extraction generation, persistence, dedupe/upsert, embeddings, and context loaders
    72→- `graph/*` contains reusable Surreal graph queries used by chat/tools and higher-level workflows.
    73→
    74→### Primary Chat Flow (`POST /api/chat/messages`)
    75→
    76→- `chat/chat-ingress.ts` validates/persists user input and registers an SSE stream message id.
    77→- `chat/chat-processor.ts` orchestrates async processing:
    78→  - load conversation + graph context
    79→  - run extraction (message and optional attachment chunks)
    80→  - persist entities/relationships/provenance
    81→  - transition onboarding state
    82→  - generate assistant response (onboarding reply or graph-aware chat)
    83→  - emit SSE events (`token`, `extraction`, `onboarding_seed`, `onboarding_state`, `assistant_message`, `done|error`)
    84→
    85→### RecordId and Table Access Rules
    86→
    87→- Use `RecordId` objects everywhere for Surreal identifiers (never `table:id` strings).
    88→- Server extraction types define typed record aliases:
    89→  - `GraphEntityRecord` and `SourceRecord` include a typed `tb` field for table discrimination.
    90→- Prefer typed `record.tb` access for table branching; do not use untyped casts like `(record as unknown as { tb: string }).tb`.
    91→
    92→## SurrealDB SDK v2
    93→
    94→Reference: https://surrealdb.com/learn/fundamentals/schemafull/define-fields
    95→
    96→- When using `http://` or `https://` URLs, the SDK uses HTTP transport only. It does NOT attempt WebSocket upgrade.
    97→- WebSocket is only used when the URL scheme is `ws://` or `wss://`.
    98→- To CREATE a record with a specific ID, use the SDK's `RecordId` class:
    99→  ```typescript
   100→  import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
   101→  const record = new RecordId("table", "my-id");
   102→  await query("CREATE $record CONTENT $content;", { record, content: {...} });
   103→  ```
   104→- To SELECT a specific record by ID, use `RecordId` in the FROM clause:
   105→  ```typescript
   106→  const record = new RecordId("gladiator", id);
   107→  await query("SELECT * FROM $record;", { record });
   108→  ```
   109→- Do NOT use string-based record IDs like `table:id` in queries - use `RecordId` objects.
   110→- For optional record fields (e.g. `option<record<match>>`), omit the field instead of setting `null` - SurrealDB defaults to `NONE`.
   111→- Query results return `RecordId` objects for record references - cast directly, no string conversion needed.
   112→- Clause order: `SELECT ... FROM ... WHERE ... LIMIT ... FETCH ...` - `LIMIT` must come before `FETCH`.
   113→- ORDER BY fields must be in the SELECT clause - you cannot order by fields not selected.
   114→- Always include every `ORDER BY` field in the `SELECT` projection. Example: if ordering decisions/questions by `created_at DESC`, select `summary, created_at` or `text, created_at` (not just `summary` / `text`).
   115→- Known issue: Surreal can throw `Expected a single result output when using the ONLY keyword` when a statement uses `ONLY` and returns no record output.
   116→- Workaround: force a return clause. In SDK calls that map to `ONLY` (for example `relate(...)`), use `.output("after")` so the statement returns a record.
   117→- Type query results directly. Access `RecordId.id` directly - do NOT create wrapper functions like `extractRecordId()`, `toString()`, etc:
   118→  ```typescript
   119→  const rows = await selectMany("SELECT id, name FROM gladiator WHERE totalWins > 0;") as Array<{ id: RecordId; name: string }>;
   120→  const result = rows.map((row) => ({ gladiatorId: row.id.id as string, name: row.name }));
   121→  ```
   122→- Do NOT use `FETCH` in queries if you need the raw RecordId reference. `FETCH` resolves references to full objects.
   123→- For `TYPE RELATION` tables, do NOT write edges with `CREATE`. `CREATE` produces non-relation records and will fail relation constraints. Use `RELATE` (or SDK `relate(...)`) to create actual relation edges.
   124→- For nested arrays (2D grids), use explicit type: `DEFINE FIELD grid ON match TYPE array<array<string>>;` - plain `array` silently rejects nested arrays in SCHEMAFULL mode.
   125→- Keep all Surreal tables in `schema/surreal-schema.surql` as `SCHEMAFULL`. Do NOT introduce `SCHEMALESS` tables.
   126→- In `SCHEMAFULL`, every persisted nested key must be explicitly declared. For `array<object>` fields, always define `field[*].subField` entries for all written properties.
   127→- Do NOT rely on permissive object inference for production payloads. If code writes a new key, update schema in the same change before deploy.
   128→- After schema changes, verify with `INFO FOR TABLE <table>;` in the target namespace/database to confirm nested fields are present.
   129→
   130→# SurrealDB Documentation References
   131→
   132→Curated links for coding agents building the AI-native business management platform. Organized by the three SurrealDB capabilities we use: graph, vector, and document.
   133→
   134→---
   135→
   136→## Getting Started
   137→
   138→- **JS/TS SDK Overview:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/sdk/javascript
   139→- **JS SDK Quick Start:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/sdk/javascript/start
   140→- **JS SDK Core Concepts (connect, auth, query):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/sdk/javascript/core
   141→- **Node.js Engine (embedded SurrealDB in Node):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/sdk/javascript/engines/node
   142→- **React Integration:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/sdk/javascript/frameworks/react
   143→- **SDK GitHub repo (v2 alpha examples):** https://github.com/surrealdb/surrealdb.js
   144→
   145→## SurrealQL Essentials
   146→
   147→- **SurrealQL Overview:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql
   148→- **SELECT:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/select
   149→- **CREATE:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/create
   150→- **UPDATE:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/update
   151→- **DELETE:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/delete
   152→- **LET (variables):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/let
   153→- **INSERT:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/insert
   154→
   155→## Schema Definition
   156→
   157→- **DEFINE TABLE:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/table
   158→- **DEFINE FIELD (types, defaults, assertions):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/field
   159→- **DEFINE INDEX (unique, full-text, vector):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/indexes
   160→- **DEFINE EVENT (triggers on record changes):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/event
   161→- **DEFINE FUNCTION (reusable SurrealQL functions):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/function
   162→
   163→## Graph Relationships (Critical for our data model)
   164→
   165→- **Graph Model Overview (best practices, when to use edges vs record links):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealdb/models/graph
   166→- **RELATE Statement (create edges):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/relate
   167→- **Graph Relations Fundamentals (RELATE, INSERT RELATION, arrow syntax):** https://surrealdb.com/learn/fundamentals/relationships/graph-relations
   168→- **Three Ways to Model Relationships (record links vs references vs graph edges):** https://surrealdb.com/blog/three-ways-to-model-data-relationships-in-surrealdb
   169→- **Graph Traversal, Recursion & Shortest Path:** https://surrealdb.com/blog/data-analysis-using-graph-traversal-recursion-and-shortest-path
   170→
   171→### Key SurrealQL graph syntax:
   172→```sql
   173→-- Create edge
   174→RELATE person:marcus->owns->project:brain;
   175→
   176→-- Traverse forward
   177→SELECT ->owns->project FROM person:marcus;
   178→
   179→-- Traverse backward
   180→SELECT <-owns<-person FROM project:brain;
   181→
   182→-- Multi-hop
   183→SELECT ->has_feature->feature->has_task->task FROM project:brain;
   184→
   185→-- Bidirectional
   186→SELECT <->conflicts_with<->decision FROM decision:d1;
   187→
   188→-- Edge with metadata
   189→RELATE decision:d1->conflicts_with->decision:d2
   190→  SET severity = 'hard', description = 'Contradictory deadlines';
   191→
   192→-- Recursive traversal (org tree, dependency chains)
   193→record:root.{..}.{ id, ->depends_on->task.@ };
   194→
   195→-- TYPE RELATION enforces edge-only tables
   196→DEFINE TABLE owns TYPE RELATION IN person OUT project | feature | task;
   197→```
   198→
   199→## Vector Search (For semantic search + RAG context)
   200→
   201→- **Vector Search Reference Guide:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealdb/reference-guide/vector-search
   202→- **DEFINE INDEX (HNSW & MTREE):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/indexes
   203→- **OpenAI Embeddings Integration:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/integrations/embeddings/openai
   204→- **Mistral Embeddings Integration:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/integrations/embeddings/mistral
   205→- **Python Embeddings (patterns transferable to JS):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/integrations/embeddings/python
   206→- **Full-Text to Vector Search Migration Guide:** https://surrealdb.com/blog/moving-from-full-text-search-to-vector-search-in-surrealdb
   207→- **Hybrid Search (vector + full-text with RRF):** https://surrealdb.com/blog/hybrid-vector-text-search-in-the-terminal-with-surrealdb-and-ratatui
   208→- **Search Functions (search::score, search::rrf):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/search
   209→- **Vector Functions (distance, similarity):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/vector
   210→
   211→### Key SurrealQL vector syntax:
   212→```sql
   213→-- Define embedding field + HNSW index
   214→DEFINE FIELD embedding ON conversation TYPE array<float>;
   215→DEFINE INDEX idx_conv_embedding ON conversation FIELDS embedding
   216→  HNSW DIMENSION 1536 DIST COSINE;
   217→
   218→-- KNN search (top 5 nearest neighbors)
   219→SELECT *, vector::similarity::cosine(embedding, $query_vec) AS similarity
   220→FROM conversation
   221→WHERE embedding <|5, COSINE|> $query_vec
   222→ORDER BY similarity DESC;
   223→
   224→-- Hybrid search (vector + full-text via RRF)
   225→LET $vs = SELECT id FROM conversation WHERE embedding <|5, COSINE|> $query_vec;
   226→LET $ft = SELECT id, search::score(1) AS score FROM conversation WHERE text @1@ 'decision about auth';
   227→RETURN search::rrf([$vs, $ft], 5);
   228→```
   229→
   230→## Real-Time & Live Queries

   233→- **LIVE SELECT:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/live
   234→
   235→Useful for pushing graph updates to the frontend in real time (new entity extracted -> graph view updates, new conflict detected -> feed updates).
   236→

   238→
   239→- **DEFINE ANALYZER:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/analyzer
   240→- **Search Functions:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/search
   241→
   242→## Useful Built-in Functions
   243→
   244→- **Time Functions (time::now, time::floor, etc.):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/time
   245→- **Array Functions (array::group, array::flatten, etc.):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/array
   246→- **String Functions:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/string
   247→- **Record Functions (record::id, record::table):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/record
   248→- **Math Functions:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/functions/database/math
   249→
   250→## Auth & Permissions
   251→
   252→- **Authentication Overview:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealdb/security/authentication
   253→- **DEFINE USER:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/user
   254→- **DEFINE TOKEN:** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealql/statements/define/token
   255→
   256→## Tools
   257→
   258→- **Surrealist (GUI client for local inspection/debugging):** https://surrealdb.com/surrealist
   259→- **CLI Reference (surreal start, import, export):** https://surrealdb.com/docs/surrealdb/cli
   260→
   261→---
   262→
   263→## Platform-Specific Patterns
   264→
   265→### Pattern: Extraction Pipeline Write
   266→After the LLM extracts entities from a message, write them to the graph in a single transaction:
   267→```sql
   268→BEGIN TRANSACTION;
   269→  -- Create entities
   270→  LET $task = CREATE task SET title = $title, status = 'open', owner = $owner;
   271→  LET $decision = CREATE decision SET summary = $summary, status = 'extracted', confidence = $conf;
   272→  
   273→  -- Create relationships
   274→  RELATE $decision->decided_in->$conversation;
   275→  RELATE $decision->belongs_to->$project;
   276→  RELATE $task->belongs_to->$feature;
   277→  RELATE $person->owns->$task;
   278→COMMIT TRANSACTION;
   279→```
   280→
   281→### Pattern: Cross-Project Conflict Detection
   282→Traverse the graph to find related entities across projects:
   283→```sql
   284→-- Find all decisions in projects that share dependencies
   285→SELECT 
   286→  id, summary, status,
   287→  <-belongs_to<-project AS projects,
   288→  ->conflicts_with->decision AS conflicts
   289→FROM decision
   290→WHERE status IN ['extracted', 'proposed', 'confirmed']
   291→  AND <-belongs_to<-project != $current_project;
   292→```
   293→
   294→### Pattern: Context Packet for MCP Server
   295→Build a token-budgeted context packet for coding agents:
   296→```sql
   297→-- Active decisions for a project
   298→SELECT summary, rationale, status, decided_at
   299→FROM decision
   300→WHERE <-belongs_to<-project = $project
   301→  AND status IN ['confirmed', 'proposed']
   302→ORDER BY decided_at DESC
   303→LIMIT 20;
   304→
   305→-- Dependency chain for current task
   306→SELECT 
   307→  id, title, status,
   308→  ->depends_on->task.{id, title, status} AS dependencies
   309→FROM task
   310→WHERE id = $current_task;
   311→```
   312→
```

  "old_string": "## Server Architecture Overview\n\n- Entrypoint is `app/server.ts`; it only calls `startServer()` from `app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts`.\n- Runtime bootstrap is split into:\n  - `runtime/config.ts` (env parsing/validation)\n  - `runtime/dependencies.ts` (Surreal + model clients)\n  - `runtime/start-server.ts` (route registration + Bun server startup)\n- HTTP cross-cutting concerns live in `app/src/server/http`:\n  - `request-logging.ts` (request context + top-level error boundary)\n  - `response.ts` (JSON/headers helpers)\n  - `parsing.ts` (request/form-data parsing)\n  - `errors.ts` + `observability.ts` (error/log primitives)\n- SSE state management is isolated in `app/src/server/streaming/sse-registry.ts`.\n- Route/business domains are separated by workflow:\n  - `workspace/*` for workspace create/bootstrap/scope checks\n  - `chat/*` for ingress + async message processing\n  - `entities/*` for entity search endpoints\n  - `onboarding/*` for onboarding state and guided replies\n  - `extraction/*` for extraction generation, persistence, dedupe/upsert, embeddings, and context loaders\n- `graph/*` contains reusable Surreal graph queries used by chat/tools and higher-level workflows.\n\n### Primary Chat Flow (`POST /api/chat/messages`)\n\n- `chat/chat-ingress.ts` validates/persists user input and registers an SSE stream message id.\n- `chat/chat-processor.ts` orchestrates async processing:\n  - load conversation + graph context\n  - run extraction (message and optional attachment chunks)\n  - persist entities/relationships/provenance\n  - transition onboarding state\n  - generate assistant response (onboarding reply or graph-aware chat)\n  - emit SSE events (`token`, `extraction`, `onboarding_seed`, `onboarding_state`, `assistant_message`, `done|error`)",
  "new_string": "## Server Architecture Overview\n\n- Entrypoint is `app/server.ts`; it only calls `startServer()` from `app/src/server/runtime/start-server.ts`.\n- Runtime bootstrap is split into:\n  - `runtime/config.ts` (env parsing/validation)\n  - `runtime/dependencies.ts` (Surreal + model clients)\n  - `runtime/start-server.ts` (route registration + Bun server startup)\n- HTTP cross-cutting concerns live in `app/src/server/http`:\n  - `request-logging.ts` (request context + top-level error boundary)\n  - `response.ts` (JSON/headers helpers)\n  - `parsing.ts` (request/form-data parsing)\n  - `errors.ts` + `observability.ts` (error/log primitives)\n- SSE state management is isolated in `app/src/server/streaming/sse-registry.ts`.\n- Route/business domains are separated by workflow:\n  - `workspace/*` for workspace create/bootstrap/scope checks\n  - `chat/*` for ingress, orchestrator, async message processing\n  - `entities/*` for entity search, detail, actions, and work item accept endpoints\n  - `onboarding/*` for onboarding state and guided replies\n  - `extraction/*` for extraction generation, persistence, dedupe/upsert, embeddings, and context loaders\n  - `agents/*` for specialized subagent implementations (PM agent)\n  - `observation/*` for observation CRUD queries\n- `graph/*` contains reusable Surreal graph queries used by chat/tools and higher-level workflows.\n\n### Orchestrator Agent Architecture\n\nThe chat system uses a thin orchestrator pattern where a single top-level agent dispatches to specialized subagents. The knowledge graph is the communication bus — agents read from and write to the graph independently, never passing data directly between each other.\n\n```\nUser Message\n  │\n  ├─→ Extraction Pipeline (always runs, Haiku)\n  │     └─→ entities/relationships → SurrealDB graph\n  │\n  └─→ Orchestrator (Sonnet, thin)\n        ├─→ Direct tools (search, entity detail, decisions, observations)\n        └─→ Subagent dispatch\n              └─→ PM Agent (Haiku) → suggestions, observations → graph\n```\n\n**Two paths to the graph:**\n| Source | Path | Why |\n|--------|------|-----|\n| User messages | Extraction pipeline (Haiku infers entities from unstructured text) | User input is unstructured |\n| Agent output | Direct graph write (agents already have structured form) | Nothing to extract |\n\n**Key files:**\n- `chat/handler.ts` — `runOrchestrator()`: streams orchestrator responses with tool use\n- `chat/context.ts` — `buildChatContext()` / `buildSystemPrompt()`: loads graph context, builds orchestrator system prompt\n- `chat/tools/index.ts` — `createOrchestratorTools()`: registers all 12 orchestrator tools\n- `chat/tools/types.ts` — `ChatToolExecutionContext`: actor-typed context (`chat_agent | mcp | orchestrator | pm_agent`)\n\n### Orchestrator Tools\n\n| Tool | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `search_entities` | Search workspace entities by text query |\n| `get_entity_detail` | Fetch entity with relationships and provenance |\n| `get_project_status` | Project task/decision/question aggregation |\n| `get_conversation_history` | Load recent conversation messages |\n| `create_provisional_decision` | Draft a decision for user review |\n| `confirm_decision` | Finalize a decision (requires explicit user auth) |\n| `resolve_decision` | Mark a decision as resolved |\n| `check_constraints` | Validate decision constraints |\n| `create_observation` | Create observation for risks/conflicts/signals |\n| `acknowledge_observation` | Mark observation as reviewed |\n| `resolve_observation` | Close a resolved observation |\n| `invoke_pm_agent` | Delegate to PM subagent |\n\n### Product Manager Subagent\n\nThe PM agent (`agents/pm/`) is the single authority on tasks, features, and project status. It is invoked by the orchestrator via `invoke_pm_agent` tool with an intent:\n\n| Intent | When to use |\n|--------|-------------|\n| `plan_work` | User discusses goals, features, or work to be done |\n| `check_status` | User asks about project status, progress, or blockers |\n| `organize` | User wants to restructure or re-prioritize |\n| `track_dependencies` | User asks about blocked items or dependency chains |\n\n**Key files:**\n- `agents/pm/agent.ts` — `runPmAgent()`: generates text with PM tools, parses strict JSON output\n- `agents/pm/prompt.ts` — `buildPmSystemPrompt()`: loads workspace projects and observations\n- `agents/pm/tools.ts` — `createPmTools()`: search_entities, get_project_status, create_observation, suggest_work_items\n- `agents/pm/suggest-work-items.ts` — Embedding-based semantic dedup (>0.97 exact duplicate, ≥0.8 merge, <0.8 new)\n\n**PM output schema:** `{ summary, suggestions: WorkItemSuggestion[], updated, discarded, observations_created }`\n\nThe orchestrator renders PM suggestions as `WorkItemSuggestionList` component blocks in the chat UI.\n\n### Observation Entity\n\nObservations (`observation/*`) are lightweight cross-cutting signals that agents write to the graph. They enable async agent-to-agent communication without forcing signals into wrong entity types.\n\n- **Severity levels:** `conflict` (contradictions needing human resolution), `warning` (risks), `info` (awareness)\n- **Lifecycle:** `open` → `acknowledged` → `resolved`\n- **Schema:** `observation` table with text, severity, status, category, source_agent, workspace, embedding\n- **Relation:** `observes` edge links observations to project/feature/task/decision/question\n- Agents load open observations as part of their context and factor them into their work.\n\n### Work Item Accept Flow\n\nWhen the PM agent suggests work items, the orchestrator renders them as `WorkItemSuggestionList` components. Users can accept or dismiss each item:\n\n- Accept calls `POST /api/workspaces/:workspaceId/work-items/accept`\n- The endpoint creates a `task` or `feature` record in SurrealDB with embedding and optional project linking\n- Implemented in `entities/work-item-accept-route.ts`\n\n### Primary Chat Flow (`POST /api/chat/messages`)\n\n- `chat/chat-ingress.ts` validates/persists user input and registers an SSE stream message id.\n- `chat/chat-processor.ts` orchestrates async processing:\n  - load conversation + graph context\n  - run extraction (message and optional attachment chunks)\n  - persist entities/relationships/provenance\n  - transition onboarding state\n  - generate assistant response (onboarding reply or orchestrator with subagent dispatch)\n  - emit SSE events (`token`, `extraction`, `onboarding_seed`, `onboarding_state`, `observation`, `assistant_message`, `done|error`)"
}
